===== order place route =====
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
import { randomInt } from 'crypto'
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { getConsumerSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/session'
import { createGuestOrderToken } from '@/lib/guest-order-token'
import { getTierPrice } from '@/lib/pricing'
import { normalizePhone } from '@/lib/phone'
import { DELIVERY_FEE_RUPEES } from '@/lib/delivery-fee'

export const runtime = 'nodejs'
export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'

type IncomingItem = { listingId: string; qty: number }

// Pragmatic email check — we only need to reject obvious junk, not enforce
// RFC 5322. The real signal is whether the buyer can be reached.
const EMAIL_RE = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/

// 4-digit handover code, generated server-side at order placement. The
// customer reads it off their order page to whoever hands over the goods —
// the rider (home delivery) or the farmer (self-pickup) — who confirms it in
// their dashboard. crypto.randomInt avoids Math.random's predictability.
function generateHandoverOtp(): string {
  return String(randomInt(0, 10000)).padStart(4, '0')
}

type ListingRow = {
  id: string
  name: string
  unit: string | null
  stock_qty: number | null
  farmer_id: string
  price_tier_1_qty: number | null
  price_tier_1_price: number | null
  price_tier_2_qty: number | null
  price_tier_2_price: number | null
  price_tier_3_price: number | null
}

const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i

function bad(msg: string, status = 400) {
  return NextResponse.json({ error: msg }, { status })
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  // No session is fine — guests may check out by providing name, email,
  // mobile, and address below (see the `guest` branch).
  const session = getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req)

  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null) as
    | {
        farmerId?: string
        paymentMethod?: string
        pickupLocation?: string | null
        items?: IncomingItem[]
        deliveryType?: string
        deliveryAddress?: string | null
        deliveryLandmark?: string | null
        deliveryPincode?: string | null
        deliveryAltPhone?: string | null
        idempotencyKey?: string | null
        guest?: { name?: string; email?: string; phone?: string } | null
      }
    | null

  if (!body) return bad('Invalid request body.')
  const { farmerId, paymentMethod, pickupLocation, items } = body
  // Optional idempotency key (a client-generated UUID per checkout attempt).
  const idempotencyKey = typeof body.idempotencyKey === 'string' && UUID_RE.test(body.idempotencyKey)
    ? body.idempotencyKey
    : null

  if (!farmerId || !UUID_RE.test(farmerId)) return bad('Invalid farmer.')
  if (paymentMethod !== 'upi' && paymentMethod !== 'cod' && paymentMethod !== 'razorpay') {
    return bad('Invalid payment method.')
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0) return bad('Cart is empty.')
  if (items.length > 50) return bad('Too many items.')
  for (const it of items) {
    if (!it || typeof it !== 'object') return bad('Invalid item.')
    if (!it.listingId || !UUID_RE.test(it.listingId)) return bad('Invalid listing id.')
    if (!Number.isFinite(it.qty) || it.qty <= 0 || it.qty > 10000) return bad('Invalid quantity.')
  }

  // Guest checkout: no session, so the buyer's identity comes from the request.
  // We still never trust the client for prices or stock — only for who they are.
  const isGuest = !session
  let guestName: string | null = null
  let guestEmail: string | null = null
  let guestPhone: string | null = null
  if (isGuest) {
    guestName = String(body.guest?.name ?? '').trim().slice(0, 80)
    guestEmail = String(body.guest?.email ?? '').trim().toLowerCase().slice(0, 200)
    guestPhone = normalizePhone(body.guest?.phone)
    if (!guestName) return bad('Please enter your name.')
    if (!EMAIL_RE.test(guestEmail)) return bad('Enter a valid email address.')
    if (!guestPhone) return bad('Enter a valid 10-digit mobile number.')
  }

  // self_pickup → buyer collects from the farm; home_delivery → our rider
  // brings it; courier → the farmer ships it themselves. Both delivery kinds
  // need a destination address.
  const deliveryType =
    body.deliveryType === 'home_delivery' ? 'home_delivery'
    : body.deliveryType === 'courier' ? 'courier'
    : 'self_pickup'
  const needsAddress = deliveryType === 'home_delivery' || deliveryType === 'courier'
  let deliveryAddress: string | null = null
  let deliveryLandmark: string | null = null
  let deliveryPincode: string | null = null
  let deliveryAltPhone: string | null = null

  if (needsAddress) {
    deliveryAddress = String(body.deliveryAddress ?? '').trim().slice(0, 400)
    deliveryLandmark = String(body.deliveryLandmark ?? '').trim().slice(0, 200) || null
    const rawPincode = String(body.deliveryPincode ?? '').trim()
    deliveryPincode = /^\d{6}$/.test(rawPincode) ? rawPincode : null
    const altPhone = normalizePhone(body.deliveryAltPhone)
    deliveryAltPhone = altPhone || null

    if (!deliveryAddress) return bad('Enter your delivery address.')
    if (deliveryAddress.length < 10) return bad('Delivery address looks too short. Please add door no, street, and area.')
    if (!deliveryPincode) return bad('Enter a valid 6-digit pincode.')
  } else if (isGuest) {
    // Guests must always give an address, even for self-pickup, so the farmer
    // has a contact/record on file. We store it in delivery_address.
    deliveryAddress = String(body.deliveryAddress ?? '').trim().slice(0, 400)
    if (!deliveryAddress) return bad('Enter your address.')
    if (deliveryAddress.length < 10) return bad('Address looks too short. Please add door no, street, and area.')
  }

  const supabase = createClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
  )

  // Resolve the authoritative buyer. For accounts we read name/phone from the
  // DB (never the client); for guests we use the values validated above and
  // leave consumer_id NULL.
  let buyerId: string | null = null
  let buyerName: string
  let buyerPhone: string
  let buyerEmail: string | null = null
  if (session) {
    const { data: consumer } = await supabase
      .from('consumers_auth')
      .select('id, name, phone')
      .eq('id', session.consumerId)
      .maybeSingle()
    if (!consumer) return bad('Account not found. Please log in again.', 401)
    buyerId = consumer.id
    buyerName = consumer.name || 'Buyer'
    buyerPhone = consumer.phone
  } else {
    buyerName = guestName || 'Buyer'
    buyerPhone = guestPhone!
    buyerEmail = guestEmail
  }

  // Idempotency: if this checkout attempt was already saved (double-tap or a
  // retry after a lost response), return the existing rows instead of placing
  // a second order and decrementing stock again. Guest orders have no
  // consumer_id, so we match on the (random UUID) key alone for them.
  if (idempotencyKey) {
    let existingQuery = supabase
      .from('orders')
      .select('id, order_code, total_price, delivery_fee')
      .eq('idempotency_key', idempotencyKey)
    existingQuery = buyerId
      ? existingQuery.eq('consumer_id', buyerId)
      : existingQuery.is('consumer_id', null)
    const { data: existing } = await existingQuery
    if (existing && existing.length > 0) {
      const existingTotal = existing.reduce((s, o) => s + (Number(o.total_price) || 0), 0)
      const existingFee = existing.reduce((s, o) => s + (Number(o.delivery_fee) || 0), 0)
      const existingIds = existing.map((r) => r.id)
      return NextResponse.json({
        ok: true,
        orderIds: existingIds,
        orderCodes: existing.map((r) => (r as { order_code?: string | null }).order_code).filter(Boolean),
        total: existingTotal,
        deliveryFee: existingFee,
        grandTotal: existingTotal + existingFee,
        deduplicated: true,
        ...(isGuest ? { guestToken=[REDACTED](existingIds) } : {}),
      })
    }
  }

  // Farmer COD acceptance check
  const { data: farmer } = await supabase
    .from('farmers')
    .select('id, cod_enabled')
    .eq('id', farmerId)
    .maybeSingle()

  if (!farmer) return bad('Farmer not found.', 404)
  if (paymentMethod === 'cod' && farmer.cod_enabled !== true) {
    return bad('This farmer is not accepting Cash on Delivery.')
  }

  // Pull live listing rows — never trust prices from the client cart
  const listingIds = items.map((i) => i.listingId)
  const { data: listings } = await supabase
    .from('produce_listings')
    .select(
      'id, name, unit, stock_qty, farmer_id, price_tier_1_qty, price_tier_1_price, price_tier_2_qty, price_tier_2_price, price_tier_3_price',
    )
    .in('id', listingIds) as { data: ListingRow[] | null }

  if (!listings || listings.length !== listingIds.length) {
    return bad('One or more items in your cart are no longer available.')
  }

  const listingById = new Map(listings.map((l) => [l.id, l]))
  const rows: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = []
  let total = 0
  // One OTP for the whole batch. Home delivery: the rider does a single
  // handover at the door. Self-pickup: the customer reads it to the farmer at
  // collection. Either way the whole checkout shares one code.
  const sharedHandoverOtp = generateHandoverOtp()
  const deliveryFee = deliveryType === 'home_delivery' ? DELIVERY_FEE_RUPEES : 0

  // Validate first (price + ownership) before we touch any stock. Stock
  // claims happen below with the RPC so two cart submits can't oversell.
  for (const item of items) {
    const listing = listingById.get(item.listingId)
    if (!listing) return bad('Item missing.')
    if (listing.farmer_id !== farmerId) return bad('Items must belong to the same farmer.')

    const unitPrice = getTierPrice(item.qty, {
      priceTier1Qty: listing.price_tier_1_qty,
      priceTier1Price: listing.price_tier_1_price,
      priceTier2Qty: listing.price_tier_2_qty,
      priceTier2Price: listing.price_tier_2_price,
      priceTier3Price: listing.price_tier_3_price,
    })

    const linePrice = unitPrice != null ? Math.round(unitPrice * item.qty) : null
    if (linePrice == null || linePrice <= 0) {
      return bad(`Price not set for ${listing.name}. Please ask the farmer.`)
    }
    total += linePrice

    rows.push({
      farmer_id: farmerId,
      produce_listing_id: listing.id,
      produce_name: listing.name,
      quantity: item.qty,
      unit: listing.unit || 'kg',
      total_price: linePrice,
      buyer_name: buyerName,
      buyer_phone: buyerPhone,
      buyer_email: buyerEmail,
      consumer_id: buyerId,
      idempotency_key: idempotencyKey,
      pickup_location: typeof pickupLocation === 'string' ? pickupLocation.slice(0, 200) : null,
      status: 'pending',
      payment_method: paymentMethod,
      payment_status: 'pending',
      delivery_type: deliveryType,
      delivery_status: deliveryType === 'home_delivery' ? 'unassigned' : null,
      delivery_address: deliveryAddress,
      delivery_landmark: deliveryLandmark,
      delivery_pincode: deliveryPincode,
      delivery_alt_phone: deliveryAltPhone,
      handover_otp: sharedHandoverOtp,
      // Fee is paid once per cart, so we stamp it on the first row only.
      // sum(delivery_fee) and sum(rider_payout) over a batch === one fee.
      delivery_fee: 0,
      rider_payout: 0,
    })
  }

  if (rows.length > 0 && deliveryFee > 0) {
    rows[0].delivery_fee = deliveryFee
    rows[0].rider_payout = deliveryFee
  }

  // Atomic stock claim. decrement_stock returns false if the listing went
  // below zero (or vanished). On any failure we revert prior claims so we
  // don't leak inventory.
  const claimed: Array<{ listingId: string; qty: number }> = []
  const revertClaims = async () => {
    for (const c of claimed) {
      try {
        await supabase.rpc('increment_stock', { p_listing_id: c.listingId, p_qty: c.qty })
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('[YFF] increment_stock revert failed:', e)
      }
    }
  }
  for (const item of items) {
    const listing = listingById.get(item.listingId)!
    const { data: ok, error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('decrement_stock', {
      p_listing_id: item.listingId,
      p_qty: item.qty,
    })
    if (rpcErr) {
      console.error('[YFF] decrement_stock rpc failed:', rpcErr.message)
      await revertClaims()
      return bad('Could not place order. Please try again.', 500)
    }
    if (!ok) {
      await revertClaims()
      return bad(`${listing.name} just sold out. Please reduce the quantity and try again.`)
    }
    claimed.push({ listingId: item.listingId, qty: item.qty })
  }

  const { data: inserted, error: insertErr } = await supabase
    .from('orders')
    .insert(rows)
    .select('id, order_code')

  if (insertErr || !inserted) {
    console.error('[YFF] place-order insert failed:', insertErr?.message)
    // Roll the stock back so the row isn't lost.
    await revertClaims()
    return bad('Could not place order. Please try again.', 500)
  }

  const orderIds = inserted.map((r) => r.id)
  return NextResponse.json({
    ok: true,
    orderIds,
    orderCodes: inserted.map((r) => (r as { order_code?: string | null }).order_code).filter(Boolean),
    total,
    deliveryFee,
    grandTotal: total + deliveryFee,
    // Guests get a short-lived token bound to these orders so they can finish
    // the Razorpay create/verify pair without a session cookie.
    ...(isGuest ? { guestToken=[REDACTED](orderIds) } : {}),
  })
}

  const { tx } = useLang()

  const [stockMsg, setStockMsg]   = useState('')
  const [adding, setAdding]       = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => { setLiveStock(item.stock_qty ?? null) }, [item.stock_qty])

  const isOutOfStock = liveStock !== null && liveStock <= 0
  const atMax        = liveStock !== null && inCart != null && inCart.qty >= liveStock

  const handleAdd = async () => {
    if (!farmer) return
    requireAuth(() => { void doAdd() })
  }

  const doAdd = async () => {
    if (!farmer) return
    setAdding(true)
    setStockMsg('')

    const { data } = await supabase
      .from('produce_listings')
      .select('stock_qty')
      .eq('id', item.id)
      .single()

    const fresh = data?.stock_qty ?? null
    if (fresh !== null) setLiveStock(fresh)
    setAdding(false)

    if (fresh !== null) {
      if (fresh <= 0) {
        setStockMsg('Out of stock / అయిపోయింది')
        return
      }
      const curQty = inCart?.qty ?? 0
      if (curQty >= fresh) {
        setStockMsg(`Maximum available / గరిష్ట పరిమాణం: ${fresh} ${unit}`)
        return
      }
      if (curQty + 1 > fresh) {
        setStockMsg(`Stock updated / స్టాక్ మారింది, only ${fresh} ${unit} available`)
        return
      }
    }

    addItem({
      listingId: item.id,
      name: item.name,
      variety: item.variety,
      emoji: item.emoji,
      unit,
      stockQty: fresh != null ? fresh : (item.stock_qty ?? undefined),
      pricePerKg: item.price_tier_1_price,
      priceTier1Qty: item.price_tier_1_qty,

      farmerId: farmer.id,
      farmerName: farmer.name,
      farmerPhone: farmer.phone,
      farmerVillage: farmer.village,
      farmerSlug: farmer.slug,
      farmerPickupLocations: farmer.pickup_locations ?? [],
    }, 1)
  }

  const handleInc = () => {
    if (liveStock !== null && inCart.qty >= liveStock) {
      setStockMsg(`Maximum available / గరిష్ట పరిమాణం: ${liveStock} ${unit}`)
      return
    }

    setQty(item.id, inCart.qty + 1)

/* ─── Location bottom sheet ─────────────────────────────────── */
function LocationBottomSheet({
  onSet,
  onClose,
}: {
  onSet: (lat: number, lng: number, name: string) => void
  onClose: () => void
}) {
  const [gpsStatus, setGpsStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'loading' | 'error'>('idle')
  const [gpsError, setGpsError]   = useState('')

  const handleGPS = () => {
    if (!navigator.geolocation) {
      setGpsError('GPS not supported on this device / GPS అందుబాటులో లేదు')
      return
    }
    setGpsStatus('loading')
    setGpsError('')
    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
      (pos) => {
        const { latitude, longitude } = pos.coords
        const name = nearestTown(latitude, longitude)
        onSet(latitude, longitude, name)
      },
      (err) => {
        setGpsStatus('error')
        setGpsError(
          err.code === 1
            ? 'Location access denied. Search your town below. / లొకేషన్ అనుమతి లేదు. పట్టణం వెతకండి.'
            : 'Could not get GPS. Search your town below. / GPS రాలేదు. పట్టణం వెతకండి.'
        )
      },
      { timeout: 12000, enableHighAccuracy: true },
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] bg-black/50 flex items-end justify-center">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-3xl p-5 space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <h2 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-lg leading-tight">
              Where are you? / మీరు ఎక్కడ ఉన్నారు?
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
              See produce from farmers near you / దగ్గరలోని రైతుల పంట చూడండి
            </p>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 text-3xl leading-none p-1">×</button>
        </div>

        {/* GPS button */}
        <button
          onClick={handleGPS}
          disabled={gpsStatus === 'loading'}
          className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl text-base active:bg-green-800 disabled:opacity-60"
        >
          {gpsStatus === 'loading' ? (
            <>
              <span className="animate-spin inline-block w-4 h-4 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full" />
              Detecting... / వెతుకుతోంది
            </>
          ) : (
            <>📍 Use my current location / నా లొకేషన్ వాడండి</>
          )}
        </button>

        {gpsError && (
          <p className="text-xs text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 leading-snug">{gpsError}</p>
        )}

        {/* Divider */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex-1 border-t border-gray-200" />
          <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-semibold">OR / లేదా</span>
          <div className="flex-1 border-t border-gray-200" />
        </div>

        {/* Search input */}
        <div>
          <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-2">
            Search your location / లొకేషన్ వెతకండి
          </label>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 mb-2">
            Type any city, town or village in AP / Telangana<br />
            ఆంధ్రప్రదేశ్ / తెలంగాణలో ఏ పట్టణమైనా టైప్ చేయండి
          </p>
          <LocationSearch
            placeholder="e.g. Visakhapatnam, Hyderabad, Eluru..."
            onSelect={(lat, lng, name) => onSet(lat, lng, name)}
          />
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="w-full text-sm text-gray-500 py-2"
        >
          Skip for now / తర్వాత చేస్తాను
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

/* ─── Demand intent banner ──────────────────────────────────── */
function DemandIntentBanner() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [crop, setCrop] = useState('')
  const [qty, setQty] = useState('')
  const [date, setDate] = useState('')
  const [location, setLocation] = useState('')
  const [name, setName] = useState('')
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    if (!crop.trim() || !name.trim() || !phone.trim()) {
      setError('Please fill crop name, your name, and phone number. / పంట పేరు, మీ పేరు, ఫోన్ నంబర్ నింపండి.')
      return
    }
    setLoading(true)
    setError('')
    const { supabase: sb } = await import('@/lib/supabase')
    const { error: err } = await sb.from('demand_intents').insert({
      crop_name: crop.trim(),
      quantity_kg: qty ? Number(qty) : null,
      needed_by_date: date || null,
      delivery_location: location.trim() || null,
      requester_name: name.trim(),
      requester_phone: phone.trim(),
      region_slug: localStorage.getItem('yff_consumer_location_name')?.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '') || 'tadepalligudem',
    })
    setLoading(false)
    if (err) { setError(err.message); return }
    setSubmitted(true)
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 mt-10 mb-4">
      <div className="bg-amber-50 border-2 border-amber-200 rounded-2xl p-4">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
          <span className="text-2xl">📣</span>
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <h3 className="font-extrabold text-amber-900 text-base leading-tight">
              Raise a Demand Intent / డిమాండ్ నమోదు
            </h3>
            <p className="text-amber-700 text-sm mt-1 leading-snug">
              Let local farmers know what you need — they&apos;ll reach out when available.
            </p>
            <p className="text-amber-600 text-xs mt-0.5">
              స్థానిక రైతులకు మీకు ఏమి కావాలో తెలియజేయండి
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {!open && !submitted && (
          <button
            onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
            className="mt-4 w-full bg-amber-600 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
          >
            <span className="text-lg leading-none">+</span>
            Raise Intent / డిమాండ్ నమోదు
          </button>
        )}

        {submitted && (
          <div className="mt-4 text-center py-3">
            <p className="text-green-700 font-bold text-base">✓ Intent raised! / నమోదైంది!</p>
            <p className="text-green-600 text-sm mt-1">
              Farmers in your area will be notified.<br />
              మీ ప్రాంతంలోని రైతులకు తెలియజేస్తాము.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {open && !submitted && (
          <div className="mt-4 space-y-3">
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Crop name * (e.g. Tomato / టమాటా)"
              value={crop}
              onChange={(e) => setCrop(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
            />
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <input
                type="number"
                placeholder="Quantity (kg) / పరిమాణం"
                value={qty}
                onChange={(e) => setQty(e.target.value)}
                className="border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
              />
              <input
                type="date"
                value={date}
                onChange={(e) => setDate(e.target.value)}
                className="border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
              />
            </div>
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Delivery location / డెలివరీ స్థలం"
              value={location}
              onChange={(e) => setLocation(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
            />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Your name * / మీ పేరు"
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
            />
            <input
              type="tel"
              placeholder="Your WhatsApp number * / మీ వాట్సాప్ నంబర్"
              value={phone}
              onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
            />

            {error && (
              <p className="text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2">{error}</p>
            )}

            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => { setOpen(false); setError('') }}
                className="flex-1 border-2 border-gray-300 text-gray-600 font-semibold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
              >
                Cancel / రద్దు
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={handleSubmit}
                disabled={loading}
                className="flex-1 bg-amber-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {loading ? 'Submitting... / నమోదు అవుతోంది' : 'Submit / నమోదు'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

===== LanguageContext =====
'use client'

import { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, ReactNode } from 'react'
import { t, Language, TranslationKey } from './translations'

type LanguageContextType = {
  lang: Language
  setLang: (lang: Language) => void
  tx: typeof t.en
  bi: (key: TranslationKey) => string
  // Pick the active language from an explicit English/Telugu pair. Used to
  // convert inline bilingual UI ("English / తెలుగు") into text that actually
  // switches with the toggle, without fragile string-splitting.
  L: (en: string, te: string) => string
}

const LanguageContext = createContext<LanguageContextType>({
  lang: 'en',
  setLang: () => {},
  tx: t.en,
  bi: (key) => t.en[key] ?? '',
  L: (en) => en,
})

export function LanguageProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [lang, setLangState] = useState<Language>('en')

  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('yff_lang') : null
    if (saved === 'en' || saved === 'te') setLangState(saved)
  }, [])

  const setLang = (next: Language) => {
    setLangState(next)
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') localStorage.setItem('yff_lang', next)
  }

  const tx = t[lang]
  const bi = (key: TranslationKey) => (t[lang] as Record<string, string>)[key] ?? t.en[key] ?? ''
  const L = (en: string, te: string) => (lang === 'te' ? te : en)

  return (
    <LanguageContext.Provider value={{ lang, setLang, tx, bi, L }}>
      {children}
    </LanguageContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function useLang() {
  return useContext(LanguageContext)
}
```

> AGENT

I've now traced all 8 items through the actual code. Here's my understanding of each, with where it lives and what I'd change. **Note:** the screenshot for the bug (#7) didn't come through to me — I can see the code path but can't see the error in your screenshot, so I've flagged my leading hypothesis and what I'd need to confirm.

---

## 1. Move "Log a Complaint" to top-right, near the profile
Right now it's a link mid-page in the consumer home: `src/app/consumer/page.tsx:269` (`href="/consumer/complaints"`, text "🛟 Log a Complaint / ఫిర్యాదు →"). The shared header/nav is `src/components/consumer/GlobalNav.tsx`.
**Understanding:** Relocate this link into the top-right of the consumer header (next to the profile/account area in `GlobalNav`) and remove the in-page one, so it's persistently reachable.

## 2. "Log a Complaint" on each individual order
Orders are listed in `src/app/consumer/orders/page.tsx`, with a per-order detail at `src/app/consumer/orders/[id]/page.tsx`. There's already a `ComplaintModal` component (`src/components/consumer/ComplaintModal.tsx`) and a `/api/consumer/complaints` endpoint.
**Understanding:** Add a per-order "Log a Complaint" action (button/link) on each order card that opens the complaint flow pre-scoped to that specific order (passing the order id), instead of the generic complaints page. I'll check whether the complaint API/schema already accepts an order reference.

## 3. "Farm Pickup Address" → "Farm Address"
The only exact label I found — "Farm pickup address / పొలం చిరునామా" — is at `src/app/farmer/dashboard/page.tsx:1477`, which is the **farmer's own profile/dashboard editor**, not the public `/farmer/[slug]` page. The public profile doesn't use that string.
**Understanding:** Change that label to "Farm Address". ⚠️ I want to confirm you mean this dashboard field (most likely), not a different "Farmer Profile" screen.

## 4. Cart: "I will pick from the farm" → "I will pick up"
In `src/components/consumer/Cart.tsx:1116` the option reads "I'll pick up from the farm / నేను తీసుకుంటాను".
**Understanding:** Change the English text to "I will pick up" (keeping the Telugu, pending item #8).

## 5. Pickup location mandatory when Pickup is selected
In `Cart.tsx`, the pickup-location dropdown (`Cart.tsx:1391`) is currently **optional** — it's not part of the `detailsMissing` gate (lines 433–436), so an order places even with no pickup point chosen.
**Understanding:** When `deliveryType === 'self_pickup'` **and** the farmer has pickup locations, require a selection — add it to `detailsMissing` so the place-order button stays disabled until one is picked.

## 6. Disable the Pickup location field for Home Delivery
Currently the pickup dropdown shows whenever the farmer *has* pickup locations (`Cart.tsx:1391`), regardless of delivery type — so it appears even for Home Delivery, which is confusing.
**Understanding:** When `deliveryType === 'home_delivery'`, disable (or hide) the pickup-location field. (This pairs with #5 — required for pickup, off for delivery.)

## 7. Bug: can't place order on Home Delivery despite filling address/pin/phone
The gate is `detailsMissing = baseDetailsMissing || deliveryDetailsMissing` (`Cart.tsx:433–436`):
- `baseDetailsMissing` = name empty **or** the **main contact phone** < 10 digits
- `deliveryDetailsMissing` = address < 10 chars **or** pincode not exactly 6 digits

**Leading hypothesis:** The "phone number" filled in the delivery form is the **Alternate phone (optional)** field (`Cart.tsx:1208`), while the **main** name/phone (the `baseDetailsMissing` check) is empty or has <10 digits — so the button stays grey and the order won't submit. Other candidates: pincode not 6 digits, or the server-side rejects (`/api/orders/place` requires address ≥10 chars and a 6-digit pincode). The server path itself looks correct for home delivery.
**I need the screenshot** (or the exact on-screen error/message) to pin down whether this is the disabled-button case or a server rejection. Can you re-attach it or paste the error text?

## 8. Product titles in selected language only (drop the "/")
Product titles render directly from the DB value — `{item.name}` in `src/components/consumer/page.tsx` and `ProduceTab.tsx` — so the "English / తెలుగు" with the "/" is baked into the stored `name`/`variety` data, not a UI helper. There's a language helper `L(en, te)` and `lang` in `src/lib/LanguageContext.tsx` already used for hardcoded UI strings.
**Understanding:** For product/variety titles, split the stored value on "/" and display only the side matching the current `lang` (English when `en`, Telugu when `te`), falling back to the whole string if there's no "/". I'd apply this wherever produce names render (consumer home, cart, order pages, farmer profile produce list) so it's consistent.

---

104	function ConsumerMenu({ name, onLogout }: { name: string | null; onLogout: () => Promise<void> }) {
105	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
106	  const wrapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
107	  const firstName = (name ?? '').split(' ')[0] || 'there'
108	
109	  useEffect(() => {
110	    if (!open) return
111	    const onDocClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {
112	      if (!wrapRef.current?.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false)
113	    }
114	    const onEsc = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') setOpen(false) }
115	    document.addEventListener('mousedown', onDocClick)
116	    document.addEventListener('keydown', onEsc)
117	    return () => {
118	      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', onDocClick)
119	      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onEsc)
120	    }
121	  }, [open])
122	
123	  return (
124	    <div ref={wrapRef} className="relative">
125	      <button
126	        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
127	        aria-haspopup="menu"
128	        aria-expanded={open}
129	        className="text-[11px] font-bold text-green-100 bg-green-800 active:bg-green-700 rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 leading-tight max-w-[104px] truncate"
130	      >
131	        Hi {firstName} ▾
132	      </button>
133	      {open && (
134	        <div
135	          role="menu"
136	          className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-56 bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl border border-gray-100 py-1.5 text-sm z-50"
137	        >
138	          <p className="px-4 py-2 text-[11px] text-gray-500 leading-tight border-b border-gray-100 mb-1">
139	            Logged in as<br />
140	            <span className="text-gray-800 font-semibold">{name || 'Consumer'}</span>
141	          </p>
142	          <Link
143	            href="/consumer/orders"
144	            onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
145	            className="block px-4 py-2.5 text-gray-800 active:bg-gray-100 font-semibold"
146	          >
147	            🧾 My orders / నా ఆర్డర్లు
148	          </Link>
149	          <Link
150	            href="/consumer"
151	            onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
152	            className="block px-4 py-2.5 text-gray-800 active:bg-gray-100"
153	          >
154	            🛒 Browse / కొనుగోలు
155	          </Link>
156	          <button
157	            onClick={() => { setOpen(false); void onLogout() }}
158	            className="block w-full text-left px-4 py-2.5 text-red-600 active:bg-red-50 font-semibold border-t border-gray-100 mt-1"
159	          >
160	            ↪ Log out / లాగౌట్
161	          </button>
162	        </div>
163	      )}
164	    </div>
165	  )
166	}

5	// Bilingual labels for the complaint categories (matches the moderator side).
6	const TYPE_OPTIONS = [
7	  { value: 'quality_complaint', label: 'Quality problem / నాణ్యత సమస్య' },
8	  { value: 'delivery_delay', label: 'Delivery delay / డెలివరీ ఆలస్యం' },
9	  { value: 'payment_issue', label: 'Payment / refund issue / చెల్లింపు సమస్య' },
10	  { value: 'other', label: 'Something else / ఇతర' },
11	]
12	
13	/**
14	 * Shared complaint form for consumers. Pass `presetOrderCode` to pin a complaint
15	 * to a specific order (the field is then locked); leave it out for a general
16	 * complaint. "Raised by" is filled server-side from the logged-in account.
17	 */
18	export default function ComplaintModal({
19	  presetOrderCode,
20	  onClose,
21	  onCreated,
22	}: {
23	  presetOrderCode?: string | null
24	  onClose: () => void
25	  onCreated: () => void
26	}) {
27	  const [type, setType] = useState('quality_complaint')
28	  const [description, setDescription] = useState('')
29	  const [orderCode, setOrderCode] = useState(presetOrderCode ?? '')
30	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
31	  const [err, setErr] = useState('')
32	  const locked = Boolean(presetOrderCode)
33	
34	  const submit = async () => {
35	    if (!description.trim()) { setErr('Please describe the problem / సమస్యను వివరించండి'); return }
36	    setSaving(true); setErr('')
37	    const r = await fetch('/api/consumer/complaints', {
38	      method: 'POST',
39	      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
40	      credentials: 'same-origin',
41	      body: JSON.stringify({ type, description: description.trim(), order_code: orderCode.trim() }),
42	    }).catch(() => null)
43	    setSaving(false)
44	    if (!r || !r.ok) {
45	      const j = r ? await r.json().catch(() => ({})) : {}
46	      setErr(j?.error ?? 'Could not submit. Try again.')
47	      return
48	    }
49	    onCreated()
50	  }
51	
52	  return (
53	    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/40 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-4" onClick={onClose}>
54	      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl w-full max-w-md p-5" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
55	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
56	          <p className="font-extrabold text-gray-900">Log a complaint / ఫిర్యాదు</p>
57	          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 text-2xl leading-none p-1">×</button>
58	        </div>
59	
60	        {err && <p className="text-xs text-red-600 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 mb-3">{err}</p>}
61	
62	        <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">Type / రకం</label>
63	        <select
64	          value={type}
65	          onChange={(e) => setType(e.target.value)}
66	          className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm mb-3 focus:outline-none focus:border-green-500"
67	        >
68	          {TYPE_OPTIONS.map((o) => <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>)}
69	        </select>
70	
71	        <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">What happened? / ఏం జరిగింది?</label>
72	        <textarea
73	          value={description}
74	          onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
75	          rows={4}
76	          autoFocus
77	          placeholder="e.g. Tomatoes arrived spoiled / ఉదా: టమోటాలు పాడైపోయాయి"
78	          className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm mb-3 focus:outline-none focus:border-green-500"
79	        />
80	
81	        <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">
82	          Order code / ఆర్డర్ కోడ్ <span className="font-normal text-gray-300">(optional)</span>
83	        </label>
84	        <input
85	          value={orderCode}
86	          onChange={(e) => setOrderCode(e.target.value)}
87	          readOnly={locked}
88	          placeholder="YFF-1042"
89	          className={`w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm mb-4 font-mono focus:outline-none focus:border-green-500 ${locked ? 'bg-gray-50 text-gray-500' : ''}`}
90	        />
91	
92	        <div className="flex gap-2">
93	          <button
94	            onClick={onClose}
95	            className="flex-1 border-2 border-gray-200 text-gray-700 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-gray-50"
96	          >
97	            Cancel / రద్దు
98	          </button>
99	          <button
100	            onClick={submit}
101	            disabled={saving}
102	            className="flex-1 bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-800 disabled:opacity-50"
103	          >
104	            {saving ? 'Sending… / పంపుతోంది' : 'Submit / సమర్పించండి'}
105	          </button>
106	        </div>
107	      </div>
108	    </div>
109	  )

1	'use client'
2	
3	import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
4	import Link from 'next/link'
5	import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'
6	import LanguageToggle from '@/components/LanguageToggle'
7	import { useConsumerAuth } from '@/lib/ConsumerAuthContext'
8	
9	type DeliveryStatus = 'unassigned' | 'assigned' | 'picked_up' | 'out_for_delivery' | 'delivered'
10	
11	type Order = {
12	  id: string
13	  order_code: string | null
14	  produce_name: string | null
15	  quantity: number | null
16	  unit: string | null
17	  total_price: number | null
18	  pickup_location: string | null
19	  status: 'pending' | 'approved' | 'declined' | 'cancelled'
20	  payment_method: string | null
21	  payment_status: string | null
22	  refund_status: string | null
23	  decline_reason: string | null
24	  payment_proof_path: string | null
25	  created_at: string
26	  farmer_id: string
27	  farmer?: {
28	    name: string
29	    slug: string
30	    village: string
31	    phone: string | null
32	    upi_id: string | null
33	  } | null
34	  delivery_type?: 'self_pickup' | 'home_delivery' | null
35	  delivery_status?: DeliveryStatus | null
36	}
37	
38	export default function ConsumerOrdersPage() {
39	  const { tx } = useLang()
40	  const { state, openAuth } = useConsumerAuth()
41	  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<Order[]>([])
42	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
43	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
44	  const [retryingId, setRetryingId] = useState<string | null>(null)
45	
46	  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
47	    setLoading(true)
48	    setError('')
49	    const r = await fetch('/api/consumer/orders', { credentials: 'same-origin' }).catch(() => null)
50	    if (!r) { setError('Could not load orders. Check your connection.'); setLoading(false); return }
51	    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))
52	    if (!r.ok) { setError(json?.error ?? 'Could not load orders.'); setLoading(false); return }
53	    setOrders((json.orders ?? []) as Order[])
54	    setLoading(false)
55	  }, [])
56	
57	  useEffect(() => {
58	    if (state.status === 'authenticated') {
59	      void refresh()
60	    } else if (state.status === 'anonymous') {
61	      setLoading(false)
62	    }
63	  }, [state.status, refresh])
64	
65	  const handleRetryPayment = async (order: Order) => {
66	    if (order.status === 'declined') return
67	    setRetryingId(order.id)
68	    const r = await fetch(`/api/orders/${order.id}/retry`, {
69	      method: 'POST',
70	      credentials: 'same-origin',
71	    }).catch(() => null)
72	    setRetryingId(null)
73	    if (!r) { setError('Network error.'); return }
74	    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))
75	    if (!r.ok) { setError(json?.error ?? 'Could not retry payment.'); return }
76	
77	    setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) =>
78	      o.id === order.id ? { ...o, payment_status: 'pending', payment_proof_path: null } : o,
79	    ))
80	    if (order.farmer?.upi_id && order.total_price) {
81	      const upiLink = `upi://pay?pa=${encodeURIComponent(order.farmer.upi_id)}&pn=${encodeURIComponent(order.farmer.name)}&am=${order.total_price}&cu=INR&tn=YourFamilyFarmer%20Order`
82	      window.location.href = upiLink
83	    }
84	  }
85	
86	  const statusColor = (s: string) =>
87	    s === 'approved'
88	      ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
89	      : s === 'declined'
90	        ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-700'
91	        : s === 'cancelled'
92	          ? 'bg-gray-200 text-gray-700'
93	          : 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800'
94	
95	  const deliveryStatusLabel = (s: DeliveryStatus | null | undefined) => {
96	    switch (s) {
97	      case 'assigned': return 'Rider assigned'
98	      case 'picked_up': return 'Picked up'
99	      case 'out_for_delivery': return 'Out for delivery'
100	      case 'delivered': return 'Delivered'
101	      default: return 'Waiting for rider'
102	    }
103	  }
104	
105	  const statusLabel = (s: string) =>
106	    s === 'approved' ? tx.statusConfirmed
107	      : s === 'declined' ? tx.statusDeclined
108	      : s === 'cancelled' ? tx.statusCancelled
109	      : tx.statusPending
110	
111	  const paymentBadge = (order: Order) => {
112	    if (!order.payment_method || order.payment_method === 'cod') return null
113	    // Online payments now go through Razorpay.
114	    if (order.payment_method === 'razorpay' && order.payment_status === 'paid') {
115	      return { label: '✓ Paid online / ఆన్‌లైన్ చెల్లించారు', cls: 'bg-green-100 text-green-800' }
116	    }
117	    // Manual UPI is retired — legacy UPI orders show no pay prompt.
118	    return null
119	  }
120	
121	  return (
122	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-16">
123	      {/* Header */}
124	      <div className="bg-green-900 px-4 pt-6 pb-10">
125	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
126	          <Link href="/consumer" className="text-green-300 text-sm flex items-center gap-1">
127	            ← Back / వెనక్కు
128	          </Link>
129	          <LanguageToggle />
130	        </div>
131	        <h1 className="text-white text-xl font-extrabold leading-tight">{tx.myOrders}</h1>
132	        <p className="text-green-400 text-sm mt-1">
133	          Your complete order history / మీ ఆర్డర్ల చరిత్ర
134	        </p>
135	      </div>
136	
137	      <div className="px-4 -mt-5 space-y-4 max-w-lg mx-auto">
138	        {state.status === 'loading' || loading ? (
139	          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-6 text-center text-sm text-gray-500">
140	            Loading... / లోడ్ అవుతోంది
141	          </div>
142	        ) : state.status === 'anonymous' ? (
143	          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-6 text-center space-y-4">
144	            <div className="text-5xl">🔒</div>
145	            <div>
146	              <p className="font-bold text-gray-900">Log in to see your orders</p>
147	              <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">
148	                మీ ఆర్డర్‌లు చూడటానికి లాగిన్ అవ్వండి
149	              </p>
150	            </div>
151	            <button
152	              onClick={openAuth}
153	              className="w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-800"
154	            >
155	              Log in / లాగిన్
156	            </button>
157	          </div>
158	        ) : error ? (
159	          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-red-100 p-6 space-y-3">
160	            <p className="text-sm text-red-600">{error}</p>
161	            <button
162	              onClick={() => void refresh()}
163	              className="text-sm font-bold text-green-700 underline"
164	            >
165	              Try again
166	            </button>
167	          </div>
168	        ) : orders.length === 0 ? (
169	          <div className="text-center py-14">
170	            <div className="text-5xl mb-3">📭</div>
171	            <p className="font-semibold text-gray-500 text-sm">{tx.noOrdersYet}</p>
172	            <Link href="/consumer" className="mt-4 inline-block text-green-700 text-sm underline font-semibold">
173	              Browse produce → / పంట చూడండి
174	            </Link>
175	          </div>
176	        ) : (
177	          <div className="space-y-3">
178	            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">
179	              {orders.length} order{orders.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''} found
180	            </p>
181	            {orders.map((order) => {
182	              const badge = paymentBadge(order)
183	              // Manual UPI flow retired — no pay-via-UPI prompt or retry on legacy orders.
184	              const needsPayment = false
185	              const canRetry = false
186	              const upiLink: string | null = null
187	              return (
188	                <Link key={order.id} href={`/consumer/orders/${order.id}`} className="block">
189	                  <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden active:bg-gray-50">
190	                    <div className="p-4 space-y-2">
191	                      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
192	                        <div className="min-w-0">
193	                          <p className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-sm leading-tight">
194	                            {order.produce_name || '—'}
195	                          </p>
196	                          {order.order_code && (
197	                            <p className="text-[11px] font-mono font-semibold text-gray-400 mt-0.5">
198	                              {order.order_code}
199	                            </p>
200	                          )}
201	                          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
202	                            {order.quantity} {order.unit || 'kg'}
203	                            {order.total_price ? ` · ₹${order.total_price}` : ''}
204	                          </p>
205	                        </div>
206	                        <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1 flex-shrink-0">
207	                          <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap ${statusColor(order.status)}`}>
208	                            {statusLabel(order.status)}
209	                          </span>
210	                          {badge && (
211	                            <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap ${badge.cls}`}>
212	                              {badge.label}
213	                            </span>
214	                          )}
215	                          {order.refund_status && order.refund_status !== 'failed' && (
216	                            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap bg-purple-100 text-purple-800">
217	                              💸 {order.refund_status === 'processed' ? 'Refunded / రీఫండ్ అయింది' : 'Refund initiated / రీఫండ్ ప్రారంభమైంది'}
218	                            </span>
219	                          )}
220	                          {order.refund_status === 'failed' && (
221	                            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap bg-red-100 text-red-800">
222	                              ⚠️ Refund failed / రీఫండ్ విఫలమైంది
223	                            </span>
224	                          )}
225	                        </div>
226	                      </div>
227	
228	                      {order.farmer && (
229	                        <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-green-700 font-semibold">
230	                          🧑‍🌾 {tx.orderedFrom} {order.farmer.name} · {order.farmer.village}
231	                        </p>
232	                      )}
233	
234	                      {order.delivery_type === 'home_delivery' ? (
235	                        <p className="text-xs text-blue-700 font-semibold">
236	                          🛵 Home delivery · {deliveryStatusLabel(order.delivery_status)}
237	                        </p>
238	                      ) : order.pickup_location ? (
239	                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">📍 {tx.pickedUpAt}: {order.pickup_location}</p>
240	                      ) : null}
241	
242	                      <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-400">
243	                        {new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', {
244	                          day: 'numeric',
245	                          month: 'short',
246	                          year: 'numeric',
247	                          hour: '2-digit',
248	                          minute: '2-digit',
249	                        })}
250	                      </p>
251	
252	                      {/* Decline reason — shown only when farmer declined */}
253	                      {order.status === 'declined' && order.decline_reason && (
254	                        <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 mt-1">
255	                          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-red-700 uppercase tracking-wide">
256	                            Reason / కారణం
257	                          </p>
258	                          <p className="text-xs text-red-800 mt-0.5 leading-snug">{order.decline_reason}</p>
259	                        </div>
260	                      )}
261	
262	                      {/* Refund message — shown when a paid order was declined */}
263	                      {order.status === 'declined' && order.refund_status === 'initiated' && (
264	                        <div className="bg-purple-50 border border-purple-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 mt-1">
265	                          <p className="text-xs text-purple-800 leading-snug">
266	                            Your payment of Rs.{order.total_price ?? 0} will be refunded to your account in 3-5 business days
267	                          </p>
268	                          <p className="text-xs text-purple-800 leading-snug mt-0.5">
269	                            మీ చెల్లింపు Rs.{order.total_price ?? 0} 3-5 పని దినాలలో తిరిగి వస్తుంది
270	                          </p>
271	                        </div>
272	                      )}
273	
274	                      {/* Pay Now button for pending UPI orders */}
275	                      {needsPayment && upiLink && (
276	                        <div className="pt-1 space-y-2">
277	                          <button
278	                            type="button"
279	                            onClick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); window.location.href = upiLink }}
280	                            className="w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-800"
281	                          >
282	                            📲 Pay ₹{order.total_price} via UPI
283	                          </button>
284	                          <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 text-center">
285	                            UPI ID: <span className="font-mono font-semibold">{order.farmer?.upi_id}</span>
286	                          </p>
287	                        </div>
288	                      )}
289	
290	                      {/* Retry payment */}
291	                      {canRetry && (
292	                        <div className="pt-1 space-y-2">
293	                          <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2">
294	                            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-800">
295	                              Farmer did not receive your payment / రైతుకు చెల్లింపు అందలేదు
296	                            </p>
297	                            <p className="text-[11px] text-amber-700 mt-0.5">
298	                              Tap retry to pay again. The farmer will be re-notified.
299	                            </p>
300	                          </div>
301	                          <button
302	                            type="button"
303	                            onClick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); void handleRetryPayment(order) }}
304	                            disabled={retryingId === order.id}
305	                            className="w-full bg-amber-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-amber-700 disabled:opacity-50"
306	                          >
307	                            {retryingId === order.id ? 'Opening UPI...' : `🔄 Retry payment ₹${order.total_price}`}
308	                          </button>
309	                        </div>
310	                      )}
311	                    </div>
312	                  </div>
313	                </Link>
314	              )
315	            })}
316	          </div>
317	        )}
318	
319	        <Link
320	          href="/consumer/complaints"
321	          className="mt-6 block text-center text-xs font-semibold text-green-700 underline"
322	        >
323	          🛟 My complaints / నా ఫిర్యాదులు
324	        </Link>
325	
326	        <Link
327	          href="/buyer-protection"
328	          className="mt-3 block text-center text-xs font-semibold text-green-700 underline"
329	        >
330	          🔒 Buyer protection & refund policy / కొనుగోలుదారు రక్షణ & రీఫండ్ విధానం
331	        </Link>
332	      </div>
333	    </main>
334	  )
335	}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/lib/localizeName.ts",
  "content": "import { Language } from './translations'\n\n/**\n * Produce names and varieties are stored bilingually as \"English / తెలుగు\".\n * Show only the side that matches the active language. When there's no \"/\"\n * separator we return the whole string unchanged, so single-language values\n * (and names that happen to contain no slash) are left alone.\n */\nexport function localizeName(value: string | null | undefined, lang: Language): string {\n  if (!value) return ''\n  const sepIdx = value.indexOf('/')\n  if (sepIdx === -1) return value.trim()\n  const en = value.slice(0, sepIdx).trim()\n  const te = value.slice(sepIdx + 1).trim()\n  return lang === 'te' ? te || en : en || te\n}\n"
}
```

Build is green (exit 0) and types pass. The remaining ESLint errors are pre-existing patterns (`window.location.href` in `handleRetryPayment`, `setState` in `fetchData` effects) — none are in lines I touched, and they don't fail the build.

## Done (7 of 8 — #7 skipped per your call)

**1. "Log a Complaint" moved to top-right near profile** — `GlobalNav.tsx`: added a 🛟 Complaint pill in the top-right cluster next to the login/profile menu; removed the old mid-hero pill from `consumer/page.tsx`.

**2. Per-order "Log a Complaint"** — `consumer/orders/page.tsx`: each order card now has a "🛟 Log a Complaint" button that opens the existing `ComplaintModal` pre-pinned to that order's code (`presetOrderCode`, so the field is locked). Used `preventDefault/stopPropagation` so it doesn't trigger the card's navigation link.

**3. "Farm Pickup Address" → "Farm Address"** — `farmer/dashboard/page.tsx:1477` (the farmer's own profile editor, as you confirmed).

**4. Cart label** — "I'll pick up from the farm" → "I will pick up" (`Cart.tsx`).

**5. Pickup location mandatory on Pickup** — `Cart.tsx`: added a per-farmer `pickupMissing` gate. When self-pickup is chosen and the farmer offers pickup points, the place-order button (both UPI and COD) stays disabled until one is selected; the field shows a red `*` and an inline "please choose" message.

**6. Pickup location disabled on Home Delivery** — the dropdown is now `disabled` and greyed whenever the delivery type isn't self-pickup (home delivery *or* courier), with a "Not needed for delivery" hint. Also guarded the API payload so a stale pickup value isn't sent for delivery orders.

**8. Single-language titles** — new helper `src/lib/localizeName.ts` splits a stored `"English / తెలుగు"` value on the first `/` and returns only the active-language side (whole string if no `/`). Applied to product/variety titles in: consumer home cards (incl. "coming soon"), cart line items, orders list + order detail (incl. receipt), farmer profile produce list, and the region browse tab.

**7. Home Delivery order bug** — skipped, as you asked. (When you re-share the error/screenshot, my leading hypothesis is the main contact name/phone being empty while only the *alternate* phone was filled, keeping the button disabled — quick to confirm and fix then.)

I haven't committed or pushed. Want me to commit these on the current branch (`feat/suspend-consumer-reason-privacy`), or should they go on a new branch?

3	import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
4	import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
5	import ModeratorShell, { useModeratorAuth } from '../ModeratorShell'
6	
7	type Farmer = {
8	  id: string
9	  slug: string
10	  name: string
11	  village: string | null
12	  district: string | null
13	  method: string | null
14	  phone: string | null
15	  active: boolean
16	  listing_count: number
17	}
18	
19	const METHOD_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
20	  natural: 'Natural',
21	  low_chemical: 'Low chem',
22	  chemical: 'Chemical',
23	}
24	
25	export default function ModeratorFarmersPage() {
26	  const router = useRouter()
27	  const { zone, checked } = useModeratorAuth()
28	  const [farmers, setFarmers] = useState<Farmer[]>([])
29	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
30	  const [busyId, setBusyId] = useState<string | null>(null)
31	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
32	
33	  const load = useCallback(async () => {
34	    const r = await fetch('/api/moderator/farmers', { credentials: 'same-origin' }).catch(() => null)
35	    setLoading(false)
36	    if (!r) { setError('Network error.'); return }
37	    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))
38	    if (!r.ok) { setError(json?.error ?? 'Could not load farmers.'); return }
39	    setFarmers((json.farmers ?? []) as Farmer[])
40	  }, [])
41	
42	  useEffect(() => { if (checked) void load() }, [checked, load])
43	
44	  const toggleActive = async (f: Farmer) => {
45	    if (busyId) return
46	    setBusyId(f.id)
47	    const r = await fetch(`/api/moderator/farmers/${f.id}`, {
48	      method: 'PATCH',
49	      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
50	      credentials: 'same-origin',
51	      body: JSON.stringify({ active: !f.active }),
52	    }).catch(() => null)
53	    setBusyId(null)
54	    if (!r || !r.ok) { setError('Update failed.'); return }
55	    setFarmers((list) => list.map((x) => (x.id === f.id ? { ...x, active: !x.active } : x)))
56	  }
57	
58	  if (!checked || !zone) {
59	    return (
60	      <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-center">
61	        <div className="w-10 h-10 border-4 border-green-700 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
62	      </main>
63	    )
64	  }
65	
66	  const activeCount = farmers.filter((f) => f.active).length
67	
68	  return (
69	    <ModeratorShell title="Farmer onboarding" subtitle="Register a new farmer or manage existing ones" zone={zone}>
70	      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
71	        <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wide">
72	          {activeCount} active farmer{activeCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''} in zone
73	        </p>
74	        <button
75	          onClick={() => router.push('/moderator/register-farmer')}
76	          className="bg-green-800 text-white text-sm font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-xl active:bg-green-900"
77	        >
78	          + Add new farmer
79	        </button>
80	      </div>
81	
82	      {error && (
83	        <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold mb-4">{error}</div>
84	      )}
85	
86	      {loading ? (
87	        <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 py-10 text-center">Loading…</p>
88	      ) : farmers.length === 0 ? (
89	        <div className="text-center py-14 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100">
90	          <div className="text-5xl mb-3">🌾</div>
91	          <p className="font-semibold text-gray-500 text-sm">No farmers yet in this zone</p>
92	        </div>
93	      ) : (
94	        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
95	          {/* header row — hidden on mobile */}
96	          <div className="hidden md:grid grid-cols-12 gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase border-b border-gray-100">
97	            <div className="col-span-4">Farmer</div>
98	            <div className="col-span-3">Village</div>
99	            <div className="col-span-2">Method</div>
100	            <div className="col-span-1 text-center">Listings</div>
101	            <div className="col-span-2 text-right">Status</div>
102	          </div>
103	          {farmers.map((f) => (
104	            <div key={f.id} className="grid grid-cols-12 gap-2 items-center px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-50 last:border-0">
105	              <div className="col-span-12 md:col-span-4 min-w-0">
106	                <p className="font-bold text-gray-900 text-sm truncate">{f.name}</p>
107	                <a href={`/farmer/${f.slug}`} target="_blank" className="text-[11px] text-green-700 underline">/farmer/{f.slug}</a>
108	              </div>
109	              <div className="col-span-6 md:col-span-3 text-sm text-gray-600 truncate">{f.village || '—'}</div>
110	              <div className="col-span-6 md:col-span-2">
111	                <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold bg-green-100 text-green-800 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
112	                  {METHOD_LABEL[f.method ?? ''] ?? f.method ?? '—'}
113	                </span>
114	              </div>
115	              <div className="col-span-4 md:col-span-1 text-sm text-gray-700 md:text-center">{f.listing_count}</div>
116	              <div className="col-span-8 md:col-span-2 flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
117	                <span className={`text-[11px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${f.active ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-500'}`}>
118	                  {f.active ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
119	                </span>
120	                <button
121	                  onClick={() => toggleActive(f)}
122	                  disabled={busyId === f.id}
123	                  className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 underline disabled:opacity-50"
124	                >
125	                  {f.active ? 'Deactivate' : 'Activate'}
126	                </button>
127	              </div>
128	            </div>
129	          ))}
130	        </div>
131	      )}
132	    </ModeratorShell>
133	  )
134	}
135

1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	import { isModeratorRequest, getModeratorZone } from '@/lib/moderator-session'
4	
5	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
6	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
7	
8	const METHODS = ['natural', 'low_chemical', 'chemical'] as const
9	
10	function svc() {
11	  return createClient(
12	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
13	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
14	  )
15	}
16	
17	// PATCH — edit farmer details or toggle active status. Scoped to the zone so a
18	// moderator can never touch a farmer outside their own region.
19	export async function PATCH(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
20	  if (!isModeratorRequest(req)) {
21	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Moderator login required.' }, { status: 401 })
22	  }
23	  const { id } = await params
24	  const zone = getModeratorZone(req)
25	  const supabase = svc()
26	
27	  // Confirm the farmer belongs to this moderator's zone before any write.
28	  const { data: farmer } = await supabase
29	    .from('farmers')
30	    .select('id, region_slug')
31	    .eq('id', id)
32	    .maybeSingle()
33	  if (!farmer || farmer.region_slug !== zone) {
34	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Farmer not found in your zone.' }, { status: 404 })
35	  }
36	
37	  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null)
38	  if (!body) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid body.' }, { status: 400 })
39	  const b = body as Record<string, unknown>
40	
41	  const update: Record<string, unknown> = {}
42	  if ('name' in b) update.name = String(b.name ?? '').trim()
43	  if ('phone' in b) update.phone = String(b.phone ?? '').trim() || null
44	  if ('village' in b) update.village = String(b.village ?? '').trim() || null
45	  if ('district' in b) update.district = String(b.district ?? '').trim() || null
46	  if ('story_quote' in b) update.story_quote = String(b.story_quote ?? '').trim() || null
47	  if ('farm_size_acres' in b) update.farm_size_acres = Number(b.farm_size_acres) || null
48	  if ('farming_since_year' in b) update.farming_since_year = Number(b.farming_since_year) || null
49	  if ('active' in b) update.active = Boolean(b.active)
50	  if ('method' in b) {
51	    const m = String(b.method ?? '')
52	    if ((METHODS as readonly string[]).includes(m)) update.method = m
53	  }
54	
55	  if (Object.keys(update).length === 0) {
56	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Nothing to update.' }, { status: 400 })
57	  }
58	
59	  const { data: updated, error } = await supabase
60	    .from('farmers')
61	    .update(update)
62	    .eq('id', id)
63	    .select('id, slug, name, phone, village, district, method, active')
64	    .single()
65	
66	  if (error) {
67	    console.error('[YFF moderator/farmers PATCH] failed:', error.message)
68	    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })
69	  }
70	
71	  return NextResponse.json({ farmer: updated })
72	}
73	
```

1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { randomInt } from 'crypto'
3	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
4	import { isModeratorRequest, getModeratorZone, getModeratorId } from '@/lib/moderator-session'
5	
6	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
7	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
8	
9	const METHODS = ['natural', 'organic', 'low_chemical', 'chemical'] as const
10	
11	// Unambiguous alphabet for activation codes — no 0/O/1/I/L so a farmer reading
12	// the code off WhatsApp can't mistype it.
13	const CODE_ALPHABET = 'REDACTED'
14	
15	function slugify(name: string): string {
16	  return name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, '')
17	}
18	
19	// A shareable activation code, e.g. "YFF-7K9Q". Caller checks DB uniqueness.
20	function makeActivationCode(): string {
21	  let s = ''
22	  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) s += CODE_ALPHABET[randomInt(CODE_ALPHABET.length)]
23	  return `YFF-${s}`
24	}
25	
26	function svc() {
27	  return createClient(
28	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
29	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
30	  )
31	}
32	
33	// GET — farmers in the moderator's zone, with a live listing count.
34	// `?mine=1` narrows to farmers this moderator personally onboarded (for the
35	// my-farmers page) and includes the activation code so it can be re-shared.
36	export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
37	  if (!isModeratorRequest(req)) {
38	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Moderator login required.' }, { status: 401 })
39	  }
40	  const zone = getModeratorZone(req)
41	  const mineOnly = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get('mine') === '1'
42	  const moderatorId = getModeratorId(req)
43	  const supabase = svc()
44	
45	  let query = supabase
46	    .from('farmers')
47	    .select('id, slug, name, village, district, method, phone, active, created_at, activation_code, registered_by_moderator')
48	    .eq('region_slug', zone)
49	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
50	
51	  if (mineOnly) {
52	    // A legacy session (no id) has no "mine" — return an empty list rather than
53	    // leaking the whole zone, prompting a re-login to populate the id.
54	    if (!moderatorId) return NextResponse.json({ farmers: [], needsRelogin: true })
55	    query = query.eq('registered_by_moderator', moderatorId)
56	  }
57	
58	  const { data: farmers, error } = await query
59	
60	  if (error) {
61	    console.error('[YFF moderator/farmers] query failed:', error.message)
62	    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })
63	  }
64	
65	  // Listing count per farmer (one grouped query, merged in JS).
66	  const ids = (farmers ?? []).map((f) => f.id)
67	  const counts: Record<string, number> = {}
68	  if (ids.length > 0) {
69	    const { data: listings } = await supabase
70	      .from('produce_listings')
71	      .select('farmer_id')
72	      .in('farmer_id', ids)
73	    for (const l of listings ?? []) {
74	      if (l.farmer_id) counts[l.farmer_id] = (counts[l.farmer_id] ?? 0) + 1
75	    }
76	  }
77	
78	  const withCounts = (farmers ?? []).map((f) => ({ ...f, listing_count: counts[f.id] ?? 0 }))
79	  return NextResponse.json({ farmers: withCounts })
80	}
81	
82	// POST — register a new farmer in the zone. Auto-generates a unique slug.
83	export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
84	  if (!isModeratorRequest(req)) {
85	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Moderator login required.' }, { status: 401 })
86	  }
87	  const zone = getModeratorZone(req)
88	  const supabase = svc()
89	
90	  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null)
91	  if (!body) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid body.' }, { status: 400 })
92	
93	  const name = String((body as { name?: unknown }).name ?? '').trim()
94	  const phone = String((body as { phone?: unknown }).phone ?? '').trim()
95	  const village = String((body as { village?: unknown }).village ?? '').trim()
96	  const district = String((body as { district?: unknown }).district ?? '').trim()
97	  const methodRaw = String((body as { method?: unknown }).method ?? 'natural').trim()
98	  const story_quote = String((body as { story_quote?: unknown }).story_quote ?? '').trim()
99	  const farm_size_acres = Number((body as { farm_size_acres?: unknown }).farm_size_acres ?? 0) || null
100	  const farming_since_year = Number((body as { farming_since_year?: unknown }).farming_since_year ?? 0) || null
101	
102	  // Marketing / payout details — the same things a farmer fills on their own
103	  // profile, so a moderator onboarding on their behalf can capture them up front.
104	  const farm_address = String((body as { farm_address?: unknown }).farm_address ?? '').trim()
105	  const upi_id = String((body as { upi_id?: unknown }).upi_id ?? '').trim()
106	  const cod_enabled = (body as { cod_enabled?: unknown }).cod_enabled === true
107	  // Bank payout + soil details collected during moderator-led onboarding.
108	  const bank_account_number = String((body as { bank_account_number?: unknown }).bank_account_number ?? '').trim()
109	  const bank_ifsc = String((body as { bank_ifsc?: unknown }).bank_ifsc ?? '').trim().toUpperCase()
110	  const socRaw = (body as { soil_organic_carbon?: unknown }).soil_organic_carbon
111	  const soil_organic_carbon = Number(socRaw) > 0 ? Number(socRaw) : null
112	
113	  // Photos & farm GPS — the same media a farmer can attach to their own profile.
114	  const cover_photo_url = String((body as { cover_photo_url?: unknown }).cover_photo_url ?? '').trim()
115	  const photo_url = String((body as { photo_url?: unknown }).photo_url ?? '').trim()
116	  const pesticide_cert_url = String((body as { pesticide_cert_url?: unknown }).pesticide_cert_url ?? '').trim()
117	  const upi_qr_code_url = String((body as { upi_qr_code_url?: unknown }).upi_qr_code_url ?? '').trim()
118	  const location_name = String((body as { location_name?: unknown }).location_name ?? '').trim()
119	  const latRaw = (body as { lat?: unknown }).lat
120	  const lngRaw = (body as { lng?: unknown }).lng
121	  const lat = typeof latRaw === 'number' && Number.isFinite(latRaw) ? latRaw : null
122	  const lng = typeof lngRaw === 'number' && Number.isFinite(lngRaw) ? lngRaw : null
123	
124	  const rawPickups = (body as { pickup_locations?: unknown }).pickup_locations
125	  const pickup_locations = Array.isArray(rawPickups)
126	    ? Array.from(new Set(rawPickups.map((p) => String(p).trim()).filter(Boolean)))
127	    : []
128	
129	  // pickup_slots: { days: string[], time_from, time_to } — stored only when at
130	  // least one day is chosen, matching the farmer-side profile editor.
131	  const slots = (body as { pickup_slots?: unknown }).pickup_slots as
132	    | { days?: unknown; time_from?: unknown; time_to?: unknown }
133	    | null
134	    | undefined
135	  const slotDays = Array.isArray(slots?.days) ? slots!.days.map((d) => String(d)).filter(Boolean) : []
136	  const pickup_slots = slotDays.length > 0
137	    ? { days: slotDays, time_from: String(slots?.time_from ?? '08:00'), time_to: String(slots?.time_to ?? '12:00') }
138	    : null
139	
140	  if (!name) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Name is required.' }, { status: 400 })
141	  if (upi_id && !/^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{2,256}@[a-zA-Z]{2,64}$/.test(upi_id)) {
142	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid UPI ID. Example: name@ybl' }, { status: 400 })
143	  }
144	  if (bank_ifsc && !/^[A-Z]{4}0[A-Z0-9]{6}$/.test(bank_ifsc)) {
145	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid IFSC. Example: SBIN0001234' }, { status: 400 })
146	  }
147	  const method = (METHODS as readonly string[]).includes(methodRaw) ? methodRaw : 'natural'
148	
149	  // Unique slug — append -2, -3, ... if taken.
150	  const base = slugify(name) || 'farmer'
151	  let slug = base
152	  for (let i = 2; i < 50; i++) {
153	    const { data: existing } = await supabase.from('farmers').select('id').eq('slug', slug).maybeSingle()
154	    if (!existing) break
155	    slug = `${base}-${i}`
156	  }
157	
158	  // Unique activation code to share with the farmer for login activation.
159	  let activation_code = makeActivationCode()
160	  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
161	    const { data: clash } = await supabase.from('farmers').select('id').eq('activation_code', activation_code).maybeSingle()
162	    if (!clash) break
163	    activation_code = makeActivationCode()
164	  }
165	
166	  // The moderator who is onboarding this farmer (from their signed session).
167	  const registered_by_moderator = getModeratorId(req)
168	
169	  const { data: inserted, error } = await supabase
170	    .from('farmers')
171	    .insert({
172	      slug,
173	      name,
174	      phone: phone || null,
175	      village: village || null,
176	      district: district || null,
177	      method,
178	      story_quote: story_quote || null,
179	      farm_size_acres,
180	      farming_since_year,
181	      farm_address: farm_address || null,
182	      pickup_locations,
183	      pickup_slots,
184	      upi_id: upi_id || null,
185	      upi_qr_code_url: upi_qr_code_url || null,
186	      cod_enabled,
187	      bank_account_number: bank_account_number || null,
188	      bank_ifsc: bank_ifsc || null,
189	      soil_organic_carbon,
190	      cover_photo_url: cover_photo_url || null,
191	      photo_url: photo_url || null,
192	      pesticide_cert_url: pesticide_cert_url || null,
193	      lat,
194	      lng,
195	      location_name: lat != null && lng != null ? (location_name || name) : null,
196	      region_slug: zone,
197	      active: true,
198	      activation_code,
199	      registered_by_moderator,
200	    })
201	    .select('id, slug, name, phone, activation_code')
202	    .single()
203	
204	  if (error) {
205	    console.error('[YFF moderator/farmers] insert failed:', error.message)
206	    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })
207	  }
208	
209	  return NextResponse.json({ farmer: inserted })
210	}
211	
```

> AGENT

I've traced the whole moderator farmer-management flow. Here's my understanding.

## Current state

- **Add** — `/moderator/register-farmer` (a big rich form) → `POST /api/moderator/farmers`. It captures a *lot*: name, phone, village, district, method, story quote, farm size, farming-since year, **farm address, pickup locations, pickup slots, UPI id + QR, COD toggle, bank account/IFSC, soil organic carbon, cover/avatar/cert photos, GPS lat/lng + location name**.
- **Deactivate/Activate** — the farmers list (`/moderator/farmers/page.tsx`) only renders a status toggle (`toggleActive` → `PATCH …/[id]` with `{ active }`).
- **No Edit UI exists** anywhere for an onboarded farmer's profile.

## The key finding

The edit *backend already half-exists*. `PATCH /api/moderator/farmers/[id]` is zone-scoped (a moderator can only touch farmers in their region) and already accepts: `name`, `phone`, `village`, `district`, `story_quote`, `farm_size_acres`, `farming_since_year`, `method`, `active`.

But it does **not** yet accept the richer fields the add form collects: `farm_address`, `pickup_locations`, `pickup_slots`, `upi_id`/`upi_qr_code_url`, `cod_enabled`, `bank_account_number`/`bank_ifsc`, `soil_organic_carbon`, photos, `lat`/`lng`/`location_name`.

## So "Edit Profile" means three pieces

1. **Entry point** — an "Edit" button in the farmers list (`/moderator/farmers`), next to Activate/Deactivate. (Possibly also on `/moderator/my-farmers`.)
2. **Edit form UI** — pre-filled with the farmer's current values, submitting via `PATCH` instead of `POST`. Cleanest is to reuse the existing register-farmer form in an "edit mode" (it already has all the field widgets + image upload), rather than duplicating ~600 lines. This also needs a way to **load the farmer's full current record** — there's currently no `GET …/[id]`, and the list GET only returns a subset, so I'd add a single-farmer GET (zone-scoped) to pre-fill.
3. **Extend the PATCH endpoint** — mirror the POST's field handling + validation (UPI/IFSC regex, pickup dedupe, slots shape, GPS) so the broader fields can actually be saved.

One nuance: slug is derived from name at creation. I'd **not** auto-change the slug on rename (it's the public profile URL `/farmer/[slug]` and changing it breaks shared links) — I'll keep slug stable unless you say otherwise.

Two decisions drive the size of this:

3	import { useState } from 'react'
4	import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
