9	type Created = { id: string; slug: string; name: string; phone: string | null; activation_code: string | null }

11	// Shrink large camera photos before upload so they stay quick on a 4G connection.
12	async function compressImage(file: File, maxPx = 800, quality = 0.7): Promise<File> {
13	  return new Promise((resolve) => {
14	    const img = new Image()
15	    const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file)
16	    img.onload = () => {
17	      URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl)
18	      const scale = Math.min(1, maxPx / Math.max(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight))
19	      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')
20	      canvas.width = Math.round(img.naturalWidth * scale)
21	      canvas.height = Math.round(img.naturalHeight * scale)
22	      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')
23	      if (!ctx) { resolve(file); return }
24	      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height)
25	      canvas.toBlob(
26	        (blob) => {
27	          if (!blob) { resolve(file); return }
28	          const name = file.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '.jpg')
29	          resolve(new File([blob], name, { type: 'image/jpeg' }))
30	        },
31	        'image/jpeg',
32	        quality,
33	      )
34	    }
35	    img.onerror = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl); resolve(file) }
36	    img.src = blobUrl
37	  })
38	}
39

61	  const [codEnabled, setCodEnabled] = useState(false)
62

72	  const [avatar, setAvatar] = useState<PhotoState>(EMPTY_PHOTO)
73	  const [cert, setCert] = useState<PhotoState>(EMPTY_PHOTO)

82	    setCover(EMPTY_PHOTO); setAvatar(EMPTY_PHOTO); setCert(EMPTY_PHOTO); setQr(EMPTY_PHOTO)
83	  }

85	  const pickPhoto = (set: (p: PhotoState) => void, current: PhotoState) => async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
86	    const file = e.target.files?.[0]
87	    if (!file) return
88	    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) { setError('Please pick an image file.'); return }
89	    if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) { setError('Image is too large (max 8 MB).'); return }
90	    setError('')
91	    if (current.preview) URL.revokeObjectURL(current.preview)
92	    const compressed = await compressImage(file)
93	    set({ file: compressed, preview: URL.createObjectURL(compressed) })
94	  }

96	    if (current.preview) URL.revokeObjectURL(current.preview)
97	    set(EMPTY_PHOTO)
98	  }
99	
100	  const handleGPS = () => {
101	    if (!navigator.geolocation) { setLocError('Geolocation not supported on this device.'); return }
102	    setLocating(true); setLocError('')
103	    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(

105	        setLat(pos.coords.latitude); setLng(pos.coords.longitude)
106	        setLocationName(form.village || 'Farm'); setLocating(false)
107	      },
108	      (err) => {

111	          : 'Could not get location. Please try again.')

113	      },
114	      { timeout: 15000, enableHighAccuracy: true },

118	  // Upload one photo to the shared farm-images bucket. The farmer row doesn't

122	    const path = `${folder}/${suffix}-${Date.now()}.${ext}`
123	    const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage

126	    if (upErr) { setError(`Upload failed: ${upErr.message}`); return null }
127	    return supabase.storage.from('farm-images').getPublicUrl(path).data.publicUrl
128	  }

133	    setPickupLocations((prev) => [...prev, v]); setNewPickup('')

388	// Success card: shows the shareable activation code (copy + WhatsApp) and the
389	// live profile link, then lets the moderator add another or view their list.
390	function FarmerCreatedCard({
391	  created,
392	  onAddAnother,
393	  onViewMine,
394	}: {
395	  created: Created
396	  onAddAnother: () => void
397	  onViewMine: () => void
398	}) {
399	  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false)
400	  const code = created.activation_code ?? ''
401	  const origin = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.origin : ''
402	  const profileUrl = `${origin}/farmer/${created.slug}`
403	
404	  const copyCode = async () => {
405	    try {
406	      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(code)
407	      setCopied(true)
408	      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1800)
409	    } catch { /* clipboard blocked — moderator can still read the code */ }
410	  }
411	
412	  const digits = (created.phone ?? '').replace(/\D/g, '')
413	  const waPhone = digits.length === 10 ? `91${digits}` : digits
414	  const waText = encodeURIComponent(
415	    `Welcome to GoGrameen, ${created.name}! 🌱\n\n`
416	    + `Your activation code: ${code}\n`
417	    + `Use it to activate your farmer login.\n\n`
418	    + `Your farm page is live: ${profileUrl}`,
419	  )
420	  const waLink = `https://wa.me/${waPhone}?text=${waText}`
421	
422	  return (
423	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-6 max-w-md">
424	      <div className="text-4xl mb-2">✅</div>
425	      <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-gray-900">{created.name} is registered</h2>
426	      <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1">Share this activation code with the farmer:</p>
427	
428	      {/* Activation code — the headline of this screen. */}
429	      <div className="mt-3 bg-green-50 border-2 border-dashed border-green-300 rounded-2xl px-4 py-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
430	        <span className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black tracking-widest text-green-900 font-mono break-all">
431	          {code || '—'}
432	        </span>
433	        <button
434	          onClick={copyCode}
435	          disabled={!code}
436	          className="bg-green-700 text-white text-xs font-bold px-3 py-2 rounded-xl active:bg-green-800 whitespace-nowrap disabled:opacity-50"
437	        >
438	          {copied ? 'Copied ✓' : 'Copy'}
439	        </button>
440	      </div>
441	
442	      <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-3">Their profile page is live:</p>
443	      <a href={`/farmer/${created.slug}`} target="_blank" className="block text-green-700 underline text-sm mt-1 break-all">{profileUrl}</a>
444	
445	      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 mt-5">
446	        {waPhone && (
447	          <a
448	            href={waLink}
449	            target="_blank"
450	            className="bg-green-600 text-white text-sm font-bold px-4 py-3 rounded-xl text-center active:bg-green-700"
451	          >
452	            Send code via WhatsApp
453	          </a>
454	        )}
455	        <button
456	          onClick={onAddAnother}
457	          className="bg-white border border-gray-200 text-gray-700 text-sm font-bold px-4 py-3 rounded-xl active:bg-gray-50"
458	        >
459	          + Register another farmer
460	        </button>
461	        <button onClick={onViewMine} className="text-gray-500 text-sm underline">
462	          View farmers I registered
463	        </button>
464	      </div>
465	    </div>
466	  )
467	}
468	
469	function PhotoUpload({
470	  preview,
471	  onPick,
472	  onClear,
473	  aspectClass,
474	}: {
475	  preview: string
476	  onPick: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void
477	  onClear: () => void
478	  aspectClass: string
479	}) {
480	  if (preview) {
481	    return (
482	      <div className={`relative ${aspectClass} rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50`}>
483	        {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
484	        <img src={preview} alt="" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
485	        <button
486	          type="button"
487	          onClick={onClear}
488	          className="absolute top-2 right-2 bg-white/90 text-gray-700 rounded-full w-7 h-7 flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-bold shadow"
489	        >
490	          ×
491	        </button>
492	      </div>
493	    )
494	  }
495	  return (
496	    <label className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border-2 border-dashed border-green-300 rounded-xl py-4 px-2 text-green-700 text-xs font-bold cursor-pointer active:bg-green-50">
497	      <span>📷</span> Add photo
498	      <input type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={onPick} className="hidden" />
499	    </label>
500	  )
501	}

1. **Extend the API** — add a `GET /api/moderator/farmers/[id]` (zone-scoped, full record) to prefill, and broaden the `PATCH` to accept the full field set with POST-equivalent validation (also fixing a latent bug: PATCH's `METHODS` is missing `'organic'`).
2. **Extract a shared `ModeratorFarmerForm`** component supporting create + edit modes (photos prefill from existing URLs).
3. **Slim register-farmer page** to use it.
4. **New edit page** `/moderator/farmers/[id]/edit`.
5. **Edit buttons** on both lists.

  "content": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { isModeratorRequest, getModeratorZone } from '@/lib/moderator-session'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\n// Keep in sync with the POST handler in ../route.ts.\nconst METHODS = ['natural', 'organic', 'low_chemical', 'chemical'] as const\n\nfunction svc() {\n  return createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n}\n\n// Columns the edit form needs to prefill — the same shape the moderator filled\n// in when onboarding the farmer.\nconst EDIT_COLUMNS =\n  'id, slug, name, phone, village, district, method, active, story_quote, ' +\n  'farm_size_acres, farming_since_year, farm_address, soil_organic_carbon, ' +\n  'upi_id, cod_enabled, bank_account_number, bank_ifsc, pickup_locations, ' +\n  'cover_photo_url, photo_url, pesticide_cert_url, upi_qr_code_url, ' +\n  'lat, lng, location_name'\n\n// GET — a single farmer's full record, scoped to the moderator's zone so the\n// edit form can prefill. Returns 404 for farmers outside the zone.\nexport async function GET(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {\n  if (!isModeratorRequest(req)) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Moderator login required.' }, { status: 401 })\n  }\n  const { id } = await params\n  const zone = getModeratorZone(req)\n  const supabase = svc()\n\n  const { data: farmer, error } = await supabase\n    .from('farmers')\n    .select(EDIT_COLUMNS)\n    .eq('id', id)\n    .maybeSingle() as { data: ({ region_slug?: string } & Record<string, unknown>) | null; error: { message: string } | null }\n\n  if (error) {\n    console.error('[YFF moderator/farmers GET] failed:', error.message)\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })\n  }\n  // We don't select region_slug above, so re-check zone with a tiny query.\n  const { data: zoneRow } = await supabase\n    .from('farmers')\n    .select('region_slug')\n    .eq('id', id)\n    .maybeSingle()\n  if (!farmer || !zoneRow || zoneRow.region_slug !== zone) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Farmer not found in your zone.' }, { status: 404 })\n  }\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ farmer })\n}\n\n// PATCH — edit farmer details or toggle active status. Scoped to the zone so a\n// moderator can never touch a farmer outside their own region. Only the fields\n// present in the body are written, so partial updates (e.g. just `active`) are\n// safe. Mirrors the validation in the POST (create) handler.\nexport async function PATCH(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {\n  if (!isModeratorRequest(req)) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Moderator login required.' }, { status: 401 })\n  }\n  const { id } = await params\n  const zone = getModeratorZone(req)\n  const supabase = svc()\n\n  // Confirm the farmer belongs to this moderator's zone before any write.\n  const { data: farmer } = await supabase\n    .from('farmers')\n    .select('id, region_slug')\n    .eq('id', id)\n    .maybeSingle()\n  if (!farmer || farmer.region_slug !== zone) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Farmer not found in your zone.' }, { status: 404 })\n  }\n\n  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null)\n  if (!body) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid body.' }, { status: 400 })\n  const b = body as Record<string, unknown>\n\n  const update: Record<string, unknown> = {}\n\n  // Core text fields.\n  if ('name' in b) {\n    const name = String(b.name ?? '').trim()\n    if (!name) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Name cannot be empty.' }, { status: 400 })\n    update.name = name\n  }\n  if ('phone' in b) update.phone = String(b.phone ?? '').trim() || null\n  if ('village' in b) update.village = String(b.village ?? '').trim() || null\n  if ('district' in b) update.district = String(b.district ?? '').trim() || null\n  if ('story_quote' in b) update.story_quote = String(b.story_quote ?? '').trim() || null\n  if ('farm_address' in b) update.farm_address = String(b.farm_address ?? '').trim() || null\n  if ('farm_size_acres' in b) update.farm_size_acres = Number(b.farm_size_acres) || null\n  if ('farming_since_year' in b) update.farming_since_year = Number(b.farming_since_year) || null\n  if ('soil_organic_carbon' in b) {\n    update.soil_organic_carbon = Number(b.soil_organic_carbon) > 0 ? Number(b.soil_organic_carbon) : null\n  }\n  if ('active' in b) update.active = Boolean(b.active)\n  if ('cod_enabled' in b) update.cod_enabled = b.cod_enabled === true\n  if ('method' in b) {\n    const m = String(b.method ?? '')\n    if ((METHODS as readonly string[]).includes(m)) update.method = m\n  }\n\n  // Payout details — validated the same way as on create.\n  if ('upi_id' in b) {\n    const upi = String(b.upi_id ?? '').trim()\n    if (upi && !/^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{2,256}@[a-zA-Z]{2,64}$/.test(upi)) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid UPI ID. Example: name@ybl' }, { status: 400 })\n    }\n    update.upi_id = upi || null\n  }\n  if ('bank_account_number' in b) {\n    update.bank_account_number = String(b.bank_account_number ?? '').trim() || null\n  }\n  if ('bank_ifsc' in b) {\n    const ifsc = String(b.bank_ifsc ?? '').trim().toUpperCase()\n    if (ifsc && !/^[A-Z]{4}0[A-Z0-9]{6}$/.test(ifsc)) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid IFSC. Example: SBIN0001234' }, { status: 400 })\n    }\n    update.bank_ifsc = ifsc || null\n  }\n\n  // Pickup locations — dedupe and drop blanks, as on create.\n  if ('pickup_locations' in b) {\n    const raw = b.pickup_locations\n    update.pickup_locations = Array.isArray(raw)\n      ? Array.from(new Set(raw.map((p) => String(p).trim()).filter(Boolean)))\n      : []\n  }\n\n  // Photos — the client sends the resolved URL (existing, newly uploaded, or\n  // null to clear).\n  if ('cover_photo_url' in b) update.cover_photo_url = String(b.cover_photo_url ?? '').trim() || null\n  if ('photo_url' in b) update.photo_url = String(b.photo_url ?? '').trim() || null\n  if ('pesticide_cert_url' in b) update.pesticide_cert_url = String(b.pesticide_cert_url ?? '').trim() || null\n  if ('upi_qr_code_url' in b) update.upi_qr_code_url = String(b.upi_qr_code_url ?? '').trim() || null\n\n  // Farm GPS. lat/lng move together; location_name follows. Clearing the\n  // location (null lat/lng) also clears the stored name.\n  if ('lat' in b || 'lng' in b) {\n    const latRaw = b.lat\n    const lngRaw = b.lng\n    const lat = typeof latRaw === 'number' && Number.isFinite(latRaw) ? latRaw : null\n    const lng = typeof lngRaw === 'number' && Number.isFinite(lngRaw) ? lngRaw : null\n    update.lat = lat\n    update.lng = lng\n    if (lat != null && lng != null) {\n      update.location_name = String(b.location_name ?? '').trim() || null\n    } else {\n      update.location_name = null\n    }\n  } else if ('location_name' in b) {\n    update.location_name = String(b.location_name ?? '').trim() || null\n  }\n\n  if (Object.keys(update).length === 0) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Nothing to update.' }, { status: 400 })\n  }\n\n  const { data: updated, error } = await supabase\n    .from('farmers')\n    .update(update)\n    .eq('id', id)\n    .select('id, slug, name, phone, village, district, method, active')\n    .single()\n\n  if (error) {\n    console.error('[YFF moderator/farmers PATCH] failed:', error.message)\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })\n  }\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ farmer: updated })\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/components/moderator/ModeratorFarmerForm.tsx",
  "content": "'use client'\n\nimport { useState } from 'react'\nimport { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'\nimport LocationSearch from '@/components/LocationSearch'\n\nexport type Created = {\n  id: string\n  slug: string\n  name: string\n  phone: string | null\n  activation_code: string | null\n}\n\n// The full set of editable fields. On create everything starts blank; on edit\n// the page passes the farmer's current values.\nexport type FarmerInitial = {\n  name: string\n  phone: string\n  village: string\n  district: string\n  method: string\n  farm_size_acres: string\n  farming_since_year: string\n  story_quote: string\n  farm_address: string\n  upi_id: string\n  soil_organic_carbon: string\n  bank_account_number: string\n  bank_ifsc: string\n  pickup_locations: string[]\n  cod_enabled: boolean\n  lat: number | null\n  lng: number | null\n  location_name: string\n  cover_photo_url: string | null\n  photo_url: string | null\n  pesticide_cert_url: string | null\n  upi_qr_code_url: string | null\n}\n\n// Shrink large camera photos before upload so they stay quick on a 4G connection.\nasync function compressImage(file: File, maxPx = 800, quality = 0.7): Promise<File> {\n  return new Promise((resolve) => {\n    const img = new Image()\n    const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file)\n    img.onload = () => {\n      URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl)\n      const scale = Math.min(1, maxPx / Math.max(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight))\n      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')\n      canvas.width = Math.round(img.naturalWidth * scale)\n      canvas.height = Math.round(img.naturalHeight * scale)\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')\n      if (!ctx) { resolve(file); return }\n      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height)\n      canvas.toBlob(\n        (blob) => {\n          if (!blob) { resolve(file); return }\n          const name = file.name.replace(/\\.[^.]+$/, '.jpg')\n          resolve(new File([blob], name, { type: 'image/jpeg' }))\n        },\n        'image/jpeg',\n        quality,\n      )\n    }\n    img.onerror = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl); resolve(file) }\n    img.src = blobUrl\n  })\n}\n\n// `file` is a freshly picked image (preview is a blob URL we must revoke);\n// `existingUrl` is a photo already saved on the farmer (edit mode). Display\n// prefers the new file's preview, falling back to the existing URL.\ntype PhotoState = { file: File | null; preview: string; existingUrl: string | null }\nconst emptyPhoto = (url: string | null = null): PhotoState => ({ file: null, preview: '', existingUrl: url })\nconst photoDisplay = (p: PhotoState) => (p.file ? p.preview : p.existingUrl ?? '')\n\nexport function emptyFarmerInitial(): FarmerInitial {\n  return {\n    name: '', phone: '', village: '', district: '',\n    method: 'natural', farm_size_acres: '', farming_since_year: '', story_quote: '',\n    farm_address: '', upi_id: '', soil_organic_carbon: '',\n    bank_account_number: '', bank_ifsc: '',\n    pickup_locations: [], cod_enabled: false,\n    lat: null, lng: null, location_name: '',\n    cover_photo_url: null, photo_url: null, pesticide_cert_url: null, upi_qr_code_url: null,\n  }\n}\n\nexport default function ModeratorFarmerForm({\n  mode,\n  farmerId,\n  initial,\n  onCreated,\n  onSaved,\n  onCancel,\n}: {\n  mode: 'create' | 'edit'\n  farmerId?: string\n  initial: FarmerInitial\n  onCreated?: (created: Created) => void\n  onSaved?: () => void\n  onCancel: () => void\n}) {\n  const [form, setForm] = useState({\n    name: initial.name, phone: initial.phone, village: initial.village, district: initial.district,\n    method: initial.method, farm_size_acres: initial.farm_size_acres,\n    farming_since_year: initial.farming_since_year, story_quote: initial.story_quote,\n    farm_address: initial.farm_address, upi_id: initial.upi_id, soil_organic_carbon: initial.soil_organic_carbon,\n    bank_account_number: initial.bank_account_number, bank_ifsc: initial.bank_ifsc,\n  })\n  const [error, setError] = useState('')\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)\n\n  const [pickupLocations, setPickupLocations] = useState<string[]>(initial.pickup_locations)\n  const [newPickup, setNewPickup] = useState('')\n  const [codEnabled, setCodEnabled] = useState(initial.cod_enabled)\n\n  const [lat, setLat] = useState<number | null>(initial.lat)\n  const [lng, setLng] = useState<number | null>(initial.lng)\n  const [locationName, setLocationName] = useState(initial.location_name)\n  const [locating, setLocating] = useState(false)\n  const [locError, setLocError] = useState('')\n\n  const [cover, setCover] = useState<PhotoState>(emptyPhoto(initial.cover_photo_url))\n  const [avatar, setAvatar] = useState<PhotoState>(emptyPhoto(initial.photo_url))\n  const [cert, setCert] = useState<PhotoState>(emptyPhoto(initial.pesticide_cert_url))\n  const [qr, setQr] = useState<PhotoState>(emptyPhoto(initial.upi_qr_code_url))\n\n  const pickPhoto = (set: (p: PhotoState) => void, current: PhotoState) => async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {\n    const file = e.target.files?.[0]\n    if (!file) return\n    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) { setError('Please pick an image file.'); return }\n    if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) { setError('Image is too large (max 8 MB).'); return }\n    setError('')\n    if (current.file && current.preview) URL.revokeObjectURL(current.preview)\n    const compressed = await compressImage(file)\n    // Picking a new image drops the previously saved URL (it'll be replaced).\n    set({ file: compressed, preview: URL.createObjectURL(compressed), existingUrl: null })\n  }\n  const clearPhoto = (set: (p: PhotoState) => void, current: PhotoState) => () => {\n    if (current.file && current.preview) URL.revokeObjectURL(current.preview)\n    set(emptyPhoto(null))\n  }\n\n  const handleGPS = () => {\n    if (!navigator.geolocation) { setLocError('Geolocation not supported on this device.'); return }\n    setLocating(true); setLocError('')\n    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(\n      (pos) => {\n        setLat(pos.coords.latitude); setLng(pos.coords.longitude)\n        setLocationName(form.village || 'Farm'); setLocating(false)\n      },\n      (err) => {\n        setLocError(err.code === 1\n          ? 'Location permission denied. Allow location in browser settings.'\n          : 'Could not get location. Please try again.')\n        setLocating(false)\n      },\n      { timeout: 15000, enableHighAccuracy: true },\n    )\n  }\n\n  const uploadPhoto = async (file: File, folder: string, suffix: string): Promise<string | null> => {\n    const ext = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || 'jpg'\n    const path = `${folder}/${suffix}-${Date.now()}.${ext}`\n    const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage\n      .from('farm-images')\n      .upload(path, file, { contentType: file.type, upsert: false })\n    if (upErr) { setError(`Upload failed: ${upErr.message}`); return null }\n    return supabase.storage.from('farm-images').getPublicUrl(path).data.publicUrl\n  }\n\n  const addPickup = () => {\n    const v = newPickup.trim()\n    if (!v || pickupLocations.includes(v)) { setNewPickup(''); return }\n    setPickupLocations((prev) => [...prev, v]); setNewPickup('')\n  }\n  const removePickup = (loc: string) => setPickupLocations((prev) => prev.filter((l) => l !== loc))\n\n  const set = (k: keyof typeof form) => (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement | HTMLSelectElement>) =>\n    setForm((f) => ({ ...f, [k]: e.target.value }))\n\n  const submit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault()\n    if (submitting) return\n    setError('')\n    setSubmitting(true)\n\n    // Upload any freshly picked photos; existing photos keep their URL, cleared\n    // ones resolve to null. Abort the whole save if an upload fails.\n    const folder = mode === 'edit' && farmerId\n      ? `farmer-${farmerId}`\n      : `onboarding-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`\n    const resolve = async (p: PhotoState, suffix: string): Promise<{ url: string | null; failed: boolean }> => {\n      if (!p.file) return { url: p.existingUrl, failed: false }\n      const url = await uploadPhoto(p.file, folder, suffix)\n      return { url, failed: url === null }\n    }\n    const [coverR, avatarR, certR, qrR] = await Promise.all([\n      resolve(cover, 'cover'),\n      resolve(avatar, 'avatar'),\n      resolve(cert, 'pesticide-cert'),\n      resolve(qr, 'upi-qr'),\n    ])\n    if (coverR.failed || avatarR.failed || certR.failed || qrR.failed) {\n      setSubmitting(false)\n      return // error already set by uploadPhoto\n    }\n\n    const payload = {\n      ...form,\n      pickup_locations: pickupLocations,\n      cod_enabled: codEnabled,\n      cover_photo_url: coverR.url,\n      photo_url: avatarR.url,\n      pesticide_cert_url: certR.url,\n      upi_qr_code_url: qrR.url,\n      lat, lng,\n      location_name: lat != null && lng != null ? (locationName || form.village) : null,\n    }\n\n    const url = mode === 'edit' && farmerId ? `/api/moderator/farmers/${farmerId}` : '/api/moderator/farmers'\n    const method = mode === 'edit' ? 'PATCH' : 'POST'\n    const r = await fetch(url, {\n      method,\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      credentials: 'same-origin',\n      body: JSON.stringify(payload),\n    }).catch(() => null)\n    setSubmitting(false)\n    if (!r) { setError('Network error.'); return }\n    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))\n    if (!r.ok || !json?.farmer) { setError(json?.error ?? 'Could not save farmer.'); return }\n\n    if (mode === 'edit') onSaved?.()\n    else onCreated?.(json.farmer as Created)\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <form onSubmit={submit} className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-5 max-w-2xl space-y-4\">\n      <div className=\"grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4\">\n        <Field label=\"Full name *\">\n          <input value={form.name} onChange={set('name')} required className={inputCls} autoFocus />\n        </Field>\n        <Field label=\"Phone (WhatsApp)\">\n          <input value={form.phone} onChange={set('phone')} placeholder=\"+91 94400 12345\" className={inputCls} />\n        </Field>\n        <Field label=\"Village\">\n          <input value={form.village} onChange={set('village')} className={inputCls} />\n        </Field>\n        <Field label=\"District\">\n          <input value={form.district} onChange={set('district')} className={inputCls} />\n        </Field>\n        <Field label=\"Farming method\">\n          <select value={form.method} onChange={set('method')} className={inputCls}>\n            <option value=\"natural\">Natural (no chemicals)</option>\n            <option value=\"organic\">Organic (certified)</option>\n            <option value=\"low_chemical\">Low chemical</option>\n            <option value=\"chemical\">Chemical</option>\n          </select>\n        </Field>\n        <Field label=\"Farm size (acres)\">\n          <input value={form.farm_size_acres} onChange={set('farm_size_acres')} type=\"number\" step=\"0.1\" className={inputCls} />\n        </Field>\n        <Field label=\"Farming since (year)\">\n          <input value={form.farming_since_year} onChange={set('farming_since_year')} type=\"number\" placeholder=\"2015\" className={inputCls} />\n        </Field>\n        <Field label=\"Soil organic carbon (%)\">\n          <input value={form.soil_organic_carbon} onChange={set('soil_organic_carbon')} type=\"number\" step=\"0.01\" placeholder=\"e.g. 0.85\" className={inputCls} />\n        </Field>\n      </div>\n      <Field label=\"Story / quote\">\n        <textarea value={form.story_quote} onChange={set('story_quote')} rows={3} className={inputCls} />\n      </Field>\n\n      {/* ── Farm location & photos — mirrors the farmer's own profile ── */}\n      <div className=\"border-t border-gray-100 pt-4\">\n        <p className=\"text-sm font-extrabold text-green-800 mb-3\">Farm location &amp; photos</p>\n\n        <div>\n          <span className=\"text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1\">Farm location (GPS)</span>\n          <p className=\"text-[11px] text-gray-500 mb-2\">Set the farm location so nearby buyers discover their produce first.</p>\n          {lat != null && lng != null ? (\n            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3\">\n              <span className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-green-800\">✓ 📍 {locationName || 'Location set'}</span>\n              <button type=\"button\" onClick={() => { setLat(null); setLng(null); setLocationName('') }} className=\"text-xs text-green-700 underline font-semibold\">Change</button>\n            </div>\n          ) : (\n            <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                onClick={handleGPS}\n                disabled={locating}\n                className=\"w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-800 disabled:opacity-50\"\n              >\n                {locating ? 'Getting location…' : '📍 Use GPS (most accurate)'}\n              </button>\n              <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                <div className=\"flex-1 border-t border-gray-200\" />\n                <span className=\"text-[10px] text-gray-400 font-semibold\">OR</span>\n                <div className=\"flex-1 border-t border-gray-200\" />\n              </div>\n              <LocationSearch\n                placeholder=\"Search farm location\"\n                onSelect={(la, ln, nm) => { setLat(la); setLng(ln); setLocationName(nm) }}\n              />\n            </div>\n          )}\n          {locError && <p className=\"text-xs text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 mt-2\">{locError}</p>}\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mt-4\">\n          <div>\n            <span className=\"text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1\">Farm cover photo</span>\n            <PhotoUpload preview={photoDisplay(cover)} onPick={pickPhoto(setCover, cover)} onClear={clearPhoto(setCover, cover)} aspectClass=\"aspect-[3/1]\" />\n          </div>\n          <div>\n            <span className=\"text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1\">Farmer photo</span>\n            <PhotoUpload preview={photoDisplay(avatar)} onPick={pickPhoto(setAvatar, avatar)} onClear={clearPhoto(setAvatar, avatar)} aspectClass=\"aspect-square max-w-[120px]\" />\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"mt-4\">\n          <span className=\"text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1\">Pesticide-free / certification photo (optional)</span>\n          <PhotoUpload preview={photoDisplay(cert)} onPick={pickPhoto(setCert, cert)} onClear={clearPhoto(setCert, cert)} aspectClass=\"aspect-[3/2] max-w-[200px]\" />\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* ── Pickup & payout — mirrors the farmer's own profile ── */}\n      <div className=\"border-t border-gray-100 pt-4\">\n        <p className=\"text-sm font-extrabold text-green-800 mb-3\">Pickup &amp; payment</p>\n\n        <Field label=\"Farm address (for pickup)\">\n          <textarea value={form.farm_address} onChange={set('farm_address')} rows={2} className={inputCls} placeholder=\"House / street, landmark, village\" />\n        </Field>\n\n        <div className=\"mt-4\">\n          <span className=\"text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1\">Pickup locations</span>\n          <div className=\"flex gap-2\">\n            <input\n              value={newPickup}\n              onChange={(e) => setNewPickup(e.target.value)}\n              onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); addPickup() } }}\n              placeholder=\"e.g. Market road junction\"\n              className={inputCls}\n            />\n            <button type=\"button\" onClick={addPickup} className=\"bg-green-700 text-white text-sm font-bold px-4 rounded-xl active:bg-green-800 whitespace-nowrap\">Add</button>\n          </div>\n          {pickupLocations.length > 0 && (\n            <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-2\">\n              {pickupLocations.map((loc) => (\n                <span key={loc} className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-green-50 border border-green-200 text-green-800 text-xs font-semibold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full\">\n                  {loc}\n                  <button type=\"button\" onClick={() => removePickup(loc)} className=\"text-green-500 text-sm leading-none\">×</button>\n                </span>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mt-4\">\n          <Field label=\"UPI ID (for payouts)\">\n            <input value={form.upi_id} onChange={set('upi_id')} placeholder=\"name@ybl\" className={inputCls} />\n          </Field>\n          <label className=\"flex items-center gap-2 mt-6\">\n            <input type=\"checkbox\" checked={codEnabled} onChange={(e) => setCodEnabled(e.target.checked)} className=\"w-4 h-4 accent-green-700\" />\n            <span className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700\">Accepts Cash on Delivery</span>\n          </label>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mt-4\">\n          <Field label=\"Bank account number\">\n            <input value={form.bank_account_number} onChange={set('bank_account_number')} inputMode=\"numeric\" placeholder=\"Account number\" className={inputCls} />\n          </Field>\n          <Field label=\"IFSC code\">\n            <input value={form.bank_ifsc} onChange={set('bank_ifsc')} placeholder=\"SBIN0001234\" className={`${inputCls} uppercase`} />\n          </Field>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"mt-4\">\n          <span className=\"text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1\">UPI QR code</span>\n          <p className=\"text-[11px] text-gray-500 mb-2\">Buyers scan this to pay. Upload a screenshot of their UPI QR.</p>\n          <PhotoUpload preview={photoDisplay(qr)} onPick={pickPhoto(setQr, qr)} onClear={clearPhoto(setQr, qr)} aspectClass=\"aspect-square max-w-[160px]\" />\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {error && (\n        <p className=\"text-xs text-red-700 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 font-semibold\">{error}</p>\n      )}\n\n      <div className=\"flex gap-2 pt-1\">\n        <button\n          type=\"submit\"\n          disabled={submitting}\n          className=\"bg-green-800 text-white text-sm font-bold px-5 py-3 rounded-xl active:bg-green-900 disabled:opacity-50\"\n        >\n          {submitting ? 'Saving…' : mode === 'edit' ? 'Save changes' : 'Register farmer'}\n        </button>\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          onClick={onCancel}\n          className=\"bg-white border border-gray-200 text-gray-700 text-sm font-bold px-5 py-3 rounded-xl active:bg-gray-50\"\n        >\n          Cancel\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </form>\n  )\n}\n\nconst inputCls = 'w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none'\n\nfunction Field({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {\n  return (\n    <label className=\"block\">\n      <span className=\"text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1\">{label}</span>\n      {children}\n    </label>\n  )\n}\n\nfunction PhotoUpload({\n  preview,\n  onPick,\n  onClear,\n  aspectClass,\n}: {\n  preview: string\n  onPick: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void\n  onClear: () => void\n  aspectClass: string\n}) {\n  if (preview) {\n    return (\n      <div className={`relative ${aspectClass} rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50`}>\n        {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}\n        <img src={preview} alt=\"\" className=\"w-full h-full object-cover\" />\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          onClick={onClear}\n          className=\"absolute top-2 right-2 bg-white/90 text-gray-700 rounded-full w-7 h-7 flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-bold shadow\"\n        >\n          ×\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    )\n  }\n  return (\n    <label className=\"flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border-2 border-dashed border-green-300 rounded-xl py-4 px-2 text-green-700 text-xs font-bold cursor-pointer active:bg-green-50\">\n      <span>📷</span> Add photo\n      <input type=\"file\" accept=\"image/*\" onChange={onPick} className=\"hidden\" />\n    </label>\n  )\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/moderator/register-farmer/page.tsx",
  "content": "'use client'\n\nimport { useState } from 'react'\nimport { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'\nimport ModeratorShell, { useModeratorAuth } from '../ModeratorShell'\nimport ModeratorFarmerForm, { emptyFarmerInitial, type Created } from '@/components/moderator/ModeratorFarmerForm'\n\nexport default function RegisterFarmerPage() {\n  const router = useRouter()\n  const { zone, checked } = useModeratorAuth()\n  const [created, setCreated] = useState<Created | null>(null)\n  // Remount the form on \"add another\" to reset all its internal state.\n  const [formKey, setFormKey] = useState(0)\n\n  if (!checked || !zone) {\n    return (\n      <main className=\"min-h-screen bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-center\">\n        <div className=\"w-10 h-10 border-4 border-green-700 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin\" />\n      </main>\n    )\n  }\n\n  // Success screen: show the activation code to share + profile link.\n  if (created) {\n    return (\n      <ModeratorShell title=\"Farmer registered\" zone={zone}>\n        <FarmerCreatedCard\n          created={created}\n          onAddAnother={() => { setCreated(null); setFormKey((k) => k + 1) }}\n          onViewMine={() => router.push('/moderator/my-farmers')}\n        />\n      </ModeratorShell>\n    )\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <ModeratorShell title=\"Register new farmer\" subtitle=\"Onboard a farmer on their behalf\" zone={zone}>\n      <ModeratorFarmerForm\n        key={formKey}\n        mode=\"create\"\n        initial={emptyFarmerInitial()}\n        onCreated={(c) => setCreated(c)}\n        onCancel={() => router.push('/moderator/farmers')}\n      />\n    </ModeratorShell>\n  )\n}\n\n// Success card: shows the shareable activation code (copy + WhatsApp) and the\n// live profile link, then lets the moderator add another or view their list.\nfunction FarmerCreatedCard({\n  created,\n  onAddAnother,\n  onViewMine,\n}: {\n  created: Created\n  onAddAnother: () => void\n  onViewMine: () => void\n}) {\n  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false)\n  const code = created.activation_code ?? ''\n  const origin = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.origin : ''\n  const profileUrl = `${origin}/farmer/${created.slug}`\n\n  const copyCode = async () => {\n    try {\n      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(code)\n      setCopied(true)\n      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1800)\n    } catch { /* clipboard blocked — moderator can still read the code */ }\n  }\n\n  const digits = (created.phone ?? '').replace(/\\D/g, '')\n  const waPhone = digits.length === 10 ? `91${digits}` : digits\n  const waText = encodeURIComponent(\n    `Welcome to GoGrameen, ${created.name}! 🌱\\n\\n`\n    + `Your activation code: ${code}\\n`\n    + `Use it to activate your farmer login.\\n\\n`\n    + `Your farm page is live: ${profileUrl}`,\n  )\n  const waLink = `https://wa.me/${waPhone}?text=${waText}`\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-6 max-w-md\">\n      <div className=\"text-4xl mb-2\">✅</div>\n      <h2 className=\"text-lg font-extrabold text-gray-900\">{created.name} is registered</h2>\n      <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1\">Share this activation code with the farmer:</p>\n\n      {/* Activation code — the headline of this screen. */}\n      <div className=\"mt-3 bg-green-50 border-2 border-dashed border-green-300 rounded-2xl px-4 py-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3\">\n        <span className=\"text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black tracking-widest text-green-900 font-mono break-all\">\n          {code || '—'}\n        </span>\n        <button\n          onClick={copyCode}\n          disabled={!code}\n          className=\"bg-green-700 text-white text-xs font-bold px-3 py-2 rounded-xl active:bg-green-800 whitespace-nowrap disabled:opacity-50\"\n        >\n          {copied ? 'Copied ✓' : 'Copy'}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 mt-3\">Their profile page is live:</p>\n      <a href={`/farmer/${created.slug}`} target=\"_blank\" className=\"block text-green-700 underline text-sm mt-1 break-all\">{profileUrl}</a>\n\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2 mt-5\">\n        {waPhone && (\n          <a\n            href={waLink}\n            target=\"_blank\"\n            className=\"bg-green-600 text-white text-sm font-bold px-4 py-3 rounded-xl text-center active:bg-green-700\"\n          >\n            Send code via WhatsApp\n          </a>\n        )}\n        <button\n          onClick={onAddAnother}\n          className=\"bg-white border border-gray-200 text-gray-700 text-sm font-bold px-4 py-3 rounded-xl active:bg-gray-50\"\n        >\n          + Register another farmer\n        </button>\n        <button onClick={onViewMine} className=\"text-gray-500 text-sm underline\">\n          View farmers I registered\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n"
}

'' : String(v))\n\nfunction toInitial(f: FarmerRow): FarmerInitial {\n  return {\n    name: str(f.name),\n    phone: str(f.phone),\n    village: str(f.village),\n    district: str(f.district),\n    method: f.method || 'natural',\n    farm_size_acres: str(f.farm_size_acres),\n    farming_since_year: str(f.farming_since_year),\n    story_quote: str(f.story_quote),\n    farm_address: str(f.farm_address),\n    upi_id: str(f.upi_id),\n    soil_organic_carbon: str(f.soil_organic_carbon),\n    bank_account_number: str(f.bank_account_number),\n    bank_ifsc: str(f.bank_ifsc),\n    pickup_locations: Array.isArray(f.pickup_locations) ? f.pickup_locations : [],\n    cod_enabled: f.cod_enabled === true,\n    lat: typeof f.lat === 'number' ? f.lat : null,\n    lng: typeof f.lng === 'number' ? f.lng : null,\n    location_name: str(f.location_name),\n    cover_photo_url: f.cover_photo_url ?? null,\n    photo_url: f.photo_url ?? null,\n    pesticide_cert_url: f.pesticide_cert_url ?? null,\n    upi_qr_code_url: f.upi_qr_code_url ?? null,\n  }\n}\n\nexport default function EditFarmerPage() {\n  const router = useRouter()\n  const params = useParams<{ id: string }>()\n  const id = params?.id\n  const { zone, checked } = useModeratorAuth()\n  const [farmer, setFarmer] = useState<FarmerRow | null>(null)\n  const [error, setError] = useState('')\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)\n\n  const load = useCallback(async () => {\n    if (!id) return\n    setLoading(true)\n    const r = await fetch(`/api/moderator/farmers/${id}`, { credentials: 'same-origin' }).catch(() => null)\n    setLoading(false)\n    if (!r) { setError('Network error.'); return }\n    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))\n    if (!r.ok) { setError(json?.error ?? 'Could not load farmer.'); return }\n    setFarmer(json.farmer as FarmerRow)\n  }, [id])\n\n  useEffect(() => { if (checked && zone) void load() }, [checked, zone, load])\n\n  if (!checked || !zone) {\n    return (\n      <main className=\"min-h-screen bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-center\">\n        <div className=\"w-10 h-10 border-4 border-green-700 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin\" />\n      </main>\n    )\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <ModeratorShell\n      title=\"Edit farmer profile\"\n      subtitle={farmer?.name ? `Editing ${farmer.name}` : 'Update an existing farmer'}\n      zone={zone}\n    >\n      {error && (\n        <div className=\"bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold mb-4\">{error}</div>\n      )}\n\n      {loading ? (\n        <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-400 py-10 text-center\">Loading…</p>\n      ) : farmer ? (\n        <ModeratorFarmerForm\n          mode=\"edit\"\n          farmerId={farmer.id}\n          initial={toInitial(farmer)}\n          onSaved={() => router.push('/moderator/farmers')}\n          onCancel={() => router.push('/moderator/farmers')}\n        />\n      ) : !error ? (\n        <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-400 py-10 text-center\">Farmer not found.</p>\n      ) : null}\n    </ModeratorShell>\n  )\n}\n"
}

Effects are intended to synchronize state between React and external systems such as manually updating the DOM, state management libraries, or other platform APIs. In general, the body of an effect should do one or both of the following:
* Update external systems with the latest state from React.
* Subscribe for updates from some external system, calling setState in a callback function when external state changes.

> AGENT

Those are the same pre-existing `set-state-in-effect` lint warnings that already apply to every moderator list page (`farmers/page.tsx:42`, `my-farmers:47` were flagged before I touched them). My new edit page deliberately follows the identical `useEffect(() => { if (checked) void load() }, ...)` pattern the codebase already uses everywhere, so it's consistent — and the production build passes (exit 0; Next doesn't treat that rule as fatal).

## Done — moderators can now edit farmer profiles

**API** (`src/app/api/moderator/farmers/[id]/route.ts`)
- Added `GET` — returns a farmer's full record, zone-scoped (404 outside the moderator's region), strips the internal `region_slug` before responding. Used to prefill the edit form.
- Broadened `PATCH` from ~9 fields to the **full profile**: farm address, soil carbon, UPI id (validated), bank account/IFSC (validated), COD toggle, pickup locations (deduped), all four photo URLs, and GPS lat/lng + location name — each only written when present, so partial updates (like the existing Activate/Deactivate) still work. Also fixed a latent bug: PATCH's `METHODS` was missing `'organic'`, so editing a farmer to organic would have silently no-op'd.

**Shared form** (`src/components/moderator/ModeratorFarmerForm.tsx`, new)
- Extracted the register form into one component with `mode: 'create' | 'edit'`. Same fields and validation for both; create → `POST`, edit → `PATCH`. Photos prefill from existing URLs and only re-upload when a new image is picked (clearing sends `null`). Slug is intentionally **not** changed on rename, so public `/farmer/[slug]` links stay valid.

**Pages**
- `register-farmer/page.tsx` slimmed to a thin wrapper around the shared form (keeps the activation-code success card; "add another" remounts the form to reset it).
- `farmers/[id]/edit/page.tsx` (new) — loads the farmer, renders the form in edit mode, returns to the list on save/cancel.
- **Edit** buttons added to both `/moderator/farmers` and `/moderator/my-farmers`, alongside the existing Activate/Deactivate.

Type-check passes; build green; new route `/moderator/farmers/[id]/edit` registered.

One thing worth a manual check when you test: confirm your `farmers` table actually has the `bank_account_number`, `bank_ifsc`, and `soil_organic_carbon` columns (the create POST already writes them, so they should exist — but the edit path now reads them back too).

- **Farmer dashboard (own profile editor)** — only a vestigial TypeScript type remains (`PickupSlots` at `farmer/dashboard/page.tsx:12-15` and the field at `:35`). There's **no input, no handler, no display** — nothing to set or edit it.

- **Cart / consumer checkout** — not shown.
- **Moderator forms** (register + the edit form I just built) — also absent; they only handle pickup **locations** (the places), not pickup **time**.

> AGENT

Type-check and build both pass. Done — pickup time is back in the farmer's own profile editor.

## What I added

Restored the original **Pickup schedule** UI in the farmer dashboard's "Edit profile" modal (it had been removed in commit `aad2150` "pickup→date"). I pulled the exact original design from git history rather than inventing a new one:

- **Day toggles** — Mon–Sun pills you tap to select collection days (`farmer/dashboard/page.tsx`, after the Pickup locations block).
- **Time window** — From / To `time` inputs (default 08:00–12:00), shown once at least one day is selected.
- **Persistence** — saves to `pickup_slots` as `{ days, time_from, time_to }`, or `null` when no day is selected (matching the existing convention).
- **Translations** — re-added the `pickupScheduleLabel` / `pickupScheduleHelp` / `pickupFrom` / `pickupTo` keys (English + Telugu) that were also removed.

The `pickup_slots` DB column and type already existed, so no migration is needed.

Two things I deliberately did **not** touch — tell me if you want either:
1. **Public display** — pickup time isn't shown on the public `/farmer/[slug]` profile or in the cart (it wasn't before removal either). I can surface it there if you want buyers to see it.
2. **Moderator forms** — the register/edit-farmer forms I built mirror the farmer profile but don't include pickup time. Want me to add the same day/time picker there too for consistency?

Want me to commit and push this?

  const whatsappVisitLink = `https://wa.me/${f.phone?.replace(/\D/g, '')}?text=${encodeURIComponent(
    tx.visitWhatsApp.replace('{name}', f.name ?? '')
  )}`

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {f.story_quote && (
        <blockquote className="bg-green-50 border-l-4 border-green-700 px-4 py-3 rounded-r-lg">
          <p className="text-sm italic text-green-900 leading-relaxed">"{f.story_quote}"</p>
          <cite className="text-xs text-green-700 font-semibold mt-2 block">— {f.name}</cite>
        </blockquote>
      )}

      <div>
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">{tx.farmDetails}</h3>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          {infoGrid.map((item) => (
            <div key={item.label} className="bg-white rounded-lg p-3 border border-gray-100">
              <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">{item.label}</p>
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 mt-0.5">{item.value}</p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg p-4 border border-gray-100">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2">{tx.howWeGrow}</h3>
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 leading-relaxed">{tx.howWeGrowDesc}</p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-3">
          {certifications.map((cert) => (
            <span key={cert} className="bg-green-100 text-green-800 text-xs font-semibold px-3 py-1 rounded-full">
              {cert}
            </span>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-amber-50 rounded-lg p-4 border border-amber-200">
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-amber-900">{tx.visitFarm}</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-amber-700 mt-1">
              {tx.visitFarmDesc} {f.farm_visit_day ?? 'Saturday morning'}. {tx.visitFarmDesc2}
            </p>
          </div>
          <span className="text-2xl">🌾</span>
        </div>
        <a
          href={whatsappVisitLink}
          target="_blank"
          rel="noopener noreferrer"
          className="mt-3 block w-full text-center bg-amber-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold py-2.5 rounded-lg"
        >
          {tx.scheduleVisit}
        </a>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

I've now traced all 11 against the live code. The key finding: **most of these are already fixed** — items 1–6 were done in this session's commits (on branch `feat/suspend-consumer-reason-privacy`, pushed but presumably not yet deployed to what the client tested), and 7/9/11 were done in earlier commits. Only **#8 is a genuine open bug**, and **#10 needs clarification**. Here's the detailed breakdown:

---

### 1. Cart: "I will pick from the farm" → "I will pick up"
**Means:** reword the self-pickup option label. **Status: ✅ Done** — `Cart.tsx:1116` now reads "I will pick up / నేను తీసుకుంటాను" (commit `5d8dd5c`). Will pass once deployed.

### 2. Pickup location mandatory when Pickup selected
**Means:** block order placement on self-pickup until a pickup point is chosen. **Status: ✅ Done** — per-farmer `pickupMissing` gate disables the place-order button + red `*`/inline error (`Cart.tsx`, commit `5d8dd5c`).

### 3. Home Delivery → Pickup location disabled
**Means:** the pickup dropdown shouldn't be usable for home delivery. **Status: ✅ Done** — dropdown is `disabled`/greyed with a "Not needed for delivery" hint whenever delivery type isn't self-pickup (commit `5d8dd5c`).

### 4. "Farm Pickup Address" → "Farm Address" on Farmer Profile
**Means:** rename the label. **Status: ✅ Done** — changed at `farmer/dashboard/page.tsx:1492` to "Farm Address / పొలం చిరునామా" (commit `5d8dd5c`). Note: the *public* `/farmer/[slug]` profile never had this label (grep is empty), so the only place it existed was the farmer's dashboard editor — which is changed.

### 5. Moderator can edit farmer profile (not just add/deactivate)
**Means:** add a full Edit Profile flow. **Status: ✅ Done** — new `GET`/broadened `PATCH` API, shared `ModeratorFarmerForm` (create+edit), edit page at `/moderator/farmers/[id]/edit`, and **Edit** buttons on both farmer lists (commit `4ef92ef`).

### 6. Titles in selected language only (remove the "/")
**Means:** stop showing "English / తెలుగు" together; show only the active language. **Status: ✅ Done** — `localizeName()` helper applied across consumer home, cart, order list/detail, farmer profile produce, region browse (commit `5d8dd5c`).

### 7. Suspension reason shown in consumer header
**Means:** moderator gives a reason when suspending a consumer; that reason appears highlighted in the consumer's header. **Status: ✅ Already implemented** — `ConsumerAuthContext` carries `suspendedReason`; `GlobalNav.tsx:90` renders a red suspension banner with the reason (commits `4b0634b` + `6e694e9`). Worth a re-test to confirm the moderator side enforces the mandatory reason.

### 8. Payment shows "Razorpay" instead of PhonePe/UPI
**Means:** buyer-facing order screens label the payment as "Razorpay" (the gateway) rather than the actual instrument. **Status: ⚠️ Real open bug.** Online orders are stored with `payment_method = 'razorpay'`, and the verify endpoint *does* save a friendly `payment_method_detail` (PhonePe/Google Pay/UPI/Card). But the display code (`consumer/orders/[id]/page.tsx`) branches on `payment_method === 'upi'` only — so for `'razorpay'` orders it falls through to printing the raw value "razorpay" (`paymentLabel()` line 246, the receipt line 519, and `paidOnline` line 671 which also breaks the timeline). **Fix:** treat `'razorpay'` as online everywhere and use `payment_method_detail` (fallback "UPI"). Not yet done.

### 9. Farmer can suspend a produce and resume it
**Means:** hide/show a listing on demand. **Status: ✅ Already implemented** — `handleToggleListingSuspend` flips `suspended_by_farmer ⇄ available` from the dashboard's inline produce list (`farmer/dashboard/page.tsx:374`). Re-test to confirm it behaves as expected.

### 10. Farmer profile shows "pickup and schedule twice" — remove one
**Means:** there's a duplicate pickup/schedule section. **Status: ❓ Needs clarification.** In the *current* code I see two *distinct* things in the profile editor — **Pickup locations** (the places) and **Pickup schedule** (days + time) — plus, separately, the order-level **pickup/delivery date** and the public profile's "Pickup/Delivery" info row and "Schedule a visit" button. I don't see a literal duplicate. The client likely tested an older build, or considers two of these redundant. I need to know *which two* they mean before removing anything.

### 11. Farmer can mention a date for pickup or delivery
**Means:** farmer sets a fulfilment date for an order. **Status: ✅ Already implemented** — on approval the farmer picks a date; `handleApprove` saves `fulfillment_date` (`farmer/dashboard/page.tsx:385`), shown to the buyer. Re-test to confirm.

---

## Net summary
- **Already fixed, just needs deploy:** 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6 (this session's branch).
- **Already working (re-test on deployed build):** 7, 9, 11.
- **Genuine work remaining:** **8** (razorpay→PhonePe/UPI label bug — clear fix).
- **Blocked on clarification:** **10** (which two sections are the duplicate?).

> AGENT

#8 fixed, committed, and pushed.

- **Branch:** `fix/payment-method-label-phonepe-upi`, commit `652da57`, build green.
- **What changed** (`consumer/orders/[id]/page.tsx`): added an `isOnlinePayment()` helper that treats both `'razorpay'` and legacy `'upi'` as online, and applied it to the three spots that previously leaked the raw "razorpay" string — the payment label, the receipt line, and the order timeline's "Payment received" step. The buyer now sees their actual instrument (PhonePe / Google Pay / UPI / Card) via `payment_method_detail`, falling back to "UPI".

**Note (separate, smaller gap):** on the *farmer* dashboard, a Razorpay order shows **no** payment badge at all (its logic only matches `'cod'`/`'upi'`). I deliberately didn't reuse the `'upi'` path there because that one triggers the manual "buyer says they paid — verify" UI, which doesn't apply to auto-verified Razorpay. Fixing it properly means adding a "✓ Paid online" badge for the farmer — happy to do that as a follow-up if you want.

This is on its own branch, not yet on `main`. Want me to:
1. **Merge `fix/...` into `main` and push** (deploys it alongside the batch you just shipped), or
2. leave it as a branch/PR for now?

And separately — ready to tackle **#10** whenever you tell me which two pickup/schedule sections look duplicated.

## After the deploy finishes (~1–2 min)
Reopen that order (`YFF-20260617-0083`) and **hard-refresh once**. The "Payment" field will then show:
- The actual instrument once known — e.g. **"PhonePe"**, "Google Pay", "UPI", or "Card" (from `payment_method_detail`).
- For a still-**pending** order like this one (payment not completed yet), it'll read **"UPI · Pending"** instead of "razorpay" — i.e. it shows the method type and status, never the gateway name.

One thing to expect: `payment_method_detail` is only filled **after** Razorpay verifies the payment. For orders that never completed payment (still ⏳ Pending, like this one), there's no real instrument to show, so it falls back to "UPI". A paid order will show the exact app (PhonePe/GPay/etc.).

Good — that completes the audit. The region page filters `status = 'available'` (line 49) and its count uses the filtered array ✓.

## Verdict on #9

**The suspended produce itself is correctly hidden from every consumer surface** — consumer home, search, region browse, and the public profile's produce list and quality list all show only `available`. So the core requirement is met.

**But I found one leak:** the public farmer profile's **"Produce now" count** (TrustStrip) is fed the raw produce array length (`slug/page.tsx:90`), which **includes suspended/paused items** — so a farmer with 3 available + 2 suspended shows "5". The listings don't appear, but the number does. Let me fix that:

> TOOL
